## 2026-11-27 - X4 locking correction (GET_LOCK + in-statement fence) IMPLEMENTED, VERIFIED on MariaDB with the real wpdb, DEPLOYED INERT; NOT committed

ADR-051 addendum (6). Lease replaced by a connection-owned advisory lock; every sync-owned DB write carries `IS_USED_LOCK(name)=CONNECTION_ID()` inside its SQL. Real-wpdb deterministic T1-T12 PASS (det10: stale write rejected, old unguarded write lands as negative control), mock 229/229, MariaDB 237/237 twice, interruption between every activation write recovers safely, scratch DB removed. Deployed (backup `/home/dreampoint.b2b/backups/x4-lock-20261127-145041/`): x4-activation fa81938c..., apros-pricing c62cadd5..., Importer 0606d4ee...; inert (`dp_x4_mode` absent, no schedule, no lock, no APROS request). TOCTOU blocker RESOLVED within the verified scope; OPEN: identity provenance (user 7), APROS confirmations, live decision. Budget: 5 h corrective (0 h extra charge), remaining approved partial 31 h.

## 2026-11-27 - X4 compact collapsible APROS notice (deployed) + TOCTOU locking review (analysis only); NOT committed

ADR-051 addendum (5). Users notice is now a collapsed `details/summary` (failures / warnings visible in the collapsed line; "Predložena odobrenja kupaca (nisu provedena)"); deployed `x4-activation.php` db4dfea7... (backup `/home/dreampoint.b2b/backups/x4-notice-20261127-134603/`), Playwright PASS (collapsed default, click + keyboard, reload, 390 px, mock failure / warning). Locking review: root cause = ownership check and write are separate statements; `wpdb::query` silently retries on errno 2006 on a NEW connection, so GET_LOCK alone is insufficient; recommended GET_LOCK + in-statement fence `IS_USED_LOCK(name)=CONNECTION_ID()` on every X4 write; estimate 5 h (corrective, includes this 1 h review). NOT implemented, awaiting owner authorization. Blockers unchanged: TOCTOU (fix designed) and circular identity corroboration. Remaining approved partial 31 h (locking correction is corrective inside the approved X4 scope, not yet counted as consumed).

Stacked, it renders TWO summaries: a native collapsible one in the sidebar (above the contact step) and the full one in `.checkout-order-summary-block-fill-wrapper` inside the actions block (after fields and terms, before the order button); the accordion was desktop-only (`isSideBySide()`), the lower heading was hidden by theme CSS. Fix: `ensureSummaryAccordion()` (JS) adds the SAME "Pregled proizvoda" toggle to whichever items wrapper is visible in the current layout (sidebar >= 992px, lower summary <= 991px), cleans the inactive copy (toggle, `data-dp-collapsed`, `id="dp-summary-items"`) and sets `data-dp-summary="lower"` on `.wc-block-checkout` only while the lower summary has its toggle; CSS then hides the native top summary with `display:none` (React tree untouched) and shows the heading "Sažetak narudžbe" (Montserrat 12/600 uppercase). One `summaryExpanded` variable keeps the state across rerenders and layout changes; collapsed by default; totals always visible. Without the attribute (script failure) both native summaries stay usable.
- **Button spacing (stacked <= 991px, `bp(md-down)`):** `.woocommerce-checkout .wc-block-checkout__actions { margin-bottom: 0 }` (the Blocks 36px margin added to the page's own padding: button-to-footer 92 -> 56px, 84 -> 48px at <= 767px) and `.woocommerce-checkout .dp-proceed { margin-top: 0 }` (the native `@container (max-width: 919px)` rule gives the lower summary wrapper `padding-bottom: 36px`, so the visible card-to-button gap is now 36px instead of 60px). Desktop >= 992px unchanged (24px button margin in the sidebar card, native 36px actions margin, 50px page padding).

### A9 - approved business rules, implementation, verification
Rules (final, owner 2026-11-25, no administrator exception): A) shipping EMPTY + usable billing -> checkbox CHECKED, effective shipping = billing, shipping form hidden; B) shipping == billing -> checked, hidden, no write; C) different shipping -> unchecked, alternative preserved; D) incomplete alternative -> unchecked, form visible; E) incomplete billing -> unchecked, form available (checkbox disabled). Checkbox = SilkyPress field `#custom-checkbox-use-billing`, label "Koristite adresu naplate za dostavu" (suffix "(neobavezno)" is Silkypress/Blocks and stays), placed directly after the company card and before the shipping step; native `.wc-block-checkout__use-address-for-billing` hidden by CSS only (state kept, billing-master protection untouched).
Implementation: `inc/checkout-logic.php` (`dreampoint_b2b_customer_address_usable()`, `dreampoint_b2b_shipping_vs_billing()`, filter `option_silkypress-input-field-block` sets label + `defaultValue`), `js/checkout-b2b-info.js` (`addressUsable`, `addressEmpty`, `syncShippingToBilling` via native `wc/store/cart.setShippingAddress`, one bounded initial sync (250 ms interval, 15 s, time-based), sync on trusted check, `checkboxTouched` guard, hide form only when checked AND shipping usable). "Usable" = street + city + country + names + postcode/state per country locale; "empty" = street, line 2, city, postcode all blank (country and the pre-selected optional state do NOT count).
Two initialization defects found and fixed after the first deploy: (1) PHP counted the pre-selected state as an address part, so the administrator session (only state + country filled) was classified "different" and the box stayed unchecked; (2) the JS initial sync gave up before React applied the default checked state (count-based retries burnt by MutationObserver calls, `checked` change produces no DOM mutation). Final deployed code verified on the administrator: checked, form hidden, store + server shipping == billing, ONE batch write, reload = 0 writes, GLS rate preserved; DB user meta untouched (session-only write).
Verified at runtime on `vis_full` (id 3) BEFORE the last two fixes: Test 1 empty, Test 2 identical (0 writes), Test 3 valid alternative, Test 4 partial alternative, Test 5 real click check/uncheck + typing into native fields (billing unchanged), Test 6 rerender stability (0 requests in 6 s), responsive 1024/768/390/320 + keyboard Space. vis_full data and cart restored exactly (verified against `vis_full-backup.json`). Test 7 (scenario E) is logic-level only (in-memory customers), 10-case matrix re-run after the fix. Delivery location (APROS) is a separate order-level field, no conflict; the SilkyPress value is saved as order meta from the same React state (never verified on a real order).

### Owner decisions / acceptance recorded
VAT number excluded permanently; contact person = billing first+last name; e-mail = billing_email; native order numbering until A7; BACS-only info card; coupons disabled; A9 rules above; "Kreni na narudzbu" wording; the owner accepted all Cart, Checkout and A9 corrections except the pending task below (latest QA); production does not exist (staging only).

## 2026-11-23 - E8 + E9 follow-up: Live Search dropdown width + account/hamburger spacing - implemented on staging, PARTIALLY VERIFIED

Root cause of the Live Search layout problem: `.search-area` is the flex-grow field inside `.action-btns` (215 px at 1024 px, the nav column is fixed), and both `#ajax-search-result` and `.search-default` were `position:absolute; width:100%` of it, so the results inherited the 215 px field width (3 px-wide title column, crowded footer). Fix (CSS only, existing markup / `js/ajax-search.js` untouched): at >=992px `.search-area` is `position:static` and `.action-btns` is the positioning anchor; dropdown and default panel are `left:0; width:min(100%, 560px)` (never wider than the action row, so no viewport overflow); 992-1199.98px: `.action-btns` gap 16px (search field 215 -> 231 px at 992-1199, "Katalog proizvoda" stays on one line), result rows padding / gaps 16px; `.product-content` `min-width:0` + `overflow-wrap:break-word`; footer `flex-wrap` + 12px gap. Mobile spacing (owner chose the Account / Hamburger pair; measured Search was never close to the hamburger): `.btn-holder` `gap:12px` (account box and hamburger used to touch), tablet `.action-btns-holder` `padding-right` 94 -> 106px. Files: `sass/components/_header.scss` (CRLF kept) and compiled `style.css` (compiled diff = only the new / changed rules). Deployed per file as `dream9399` (baseline hashes verified first: staging `style.css` == Git HEAD), SHA-256 parity verified; backup / rollback `/home/dreampoint.b2b/backups/search-mobile-20261123-150807/` (restore `style.css` and `sass/components/_header.scss`). Verified on the authenticated live staging page (DOM geometry, query `sd`, 3 results, plus empty-state panel and "no results" state at 1024; long title simulated in the DOM only, no data changed): 992 / 1024 / 1100 / 1199: dropdown 440 px, input 231 px, footer buttons fully visible, no overflow, Katalog on one line; 1200: dropdown 519 px (input 294); 1440: 560 px (input 352); 991 / 900 / 820 / 768 / 390 / 360: order Search | Quick Order | Cart | Account | Hamburger (tablet) intact, account -> hamburger gap 12px, no horizontal overflow, phone bottom bar positions unchanged, no console errors. NOT verified: real mouse / keyboard / touch on the live page (the extension tab is hidden; live search was triggered by dispatched input events), long product titles on real data, other browsers, the mobile search overlay (not changed, `md-down` rules untouched). **768-991px icon consistency (owner follow-up):** at 768px Search and Cart are 32px icon-font glyphs while `icon-user` (Account) was 24px with more internal padding, so it looked about 25% smaller; fixed with one rule `#header .btn-holder .mobile-account-link { font-size: 32px }` at 768-991.98px only (44x44 box, 12px gap, order and the hamburger SVG unchanged; phones keep 24px). ## 2026-11-20 - A12 read-only RCA complete; four owner requirements recorded (E11, E12, E7-C, A13) - DOCUMENTATION ONLY

Baseline: local `master` = `origin/master` = `652abd9`, clean tree. Nothing implemented, imported, saved, deployed, purged or configured; staging was touched only by read-only `wp eval-file` queries and `grep` over SSH (temporary helper scripts in `/tmp` on the server were removed afterwards).

- **A12 (P1) - isolated, not systematic.** Staging, 451 published products (251 simple + 200 variable): exactly two have no price, the two reported Nuuna notebooks (posts 7576 / 7582, ERP ids 53084 / 53085, simple, in stock, ERP-managed). They have no `_price` / `_regular_price` / `_sale_price` rows at all. Failing layer: ERP source / import mapping. `AprosProvider` resolves `wholesalePrice ?? priceWithVat ?? priceWithoutVat ?? 0` and `Importer` skips every price write when the resolved price is falsy, so a missing APROS wholesale price silently yields a published, unpriced, non-purchasable product. `apros-pricing` cannot supply a price (0 country-price rows for both articles; brand discount needs `regular > 0`). Evaluated as `vis_full` (base context, partner code 0). Confidence HIGH for the layer, MEDIUM-HIGH that APROS holds no wholesale price (raw payload not inspected; no persisted importer log). Remediation (owner approval needed): confirm the price in APROS, then an owner-approved ordinary import; no code change; interim visibility is an owner decision. Full record and regression plan: `master-roadmap.md` row A12. A12 stays OPEN.
- **Owner requirements recorded exactly once:** item 1 Quick Cart attributes -> **E11** (P3, new); item 2 Quick Cart not refreshing after the Quick Order footer add-to-cart -> **E12** (P2, new; existing `wc_fragment_refresh` bridge in `quick-order.js:104` should already refresh it, cause NOT established, five hypotheses listed); item 3 sticky `.cart-collaterals` -> **E7 Sub-item C** (existing task reused, P3, deferred); item 4 Croatian terms checkbox -> **A13** (P2, new; source = WooCommerce Blocks terms block + hr language pack + page titles, so a pure translation fix is impossible; options and recommendation recorded).
- X22: E11 / E12 touch `mini-cart.php` (row 2), E7-C may touch `cart.php` (row 1) -> do inside the matching X22 Phase B passes; A13 is independent of X22.
- Not reopened: C5, E1, T5, T6. No ADR added (no architectural decision made).

The filter sidebar was the first slice (Figma QO-01 `11148:33784`, QO-02 `39288`); the results toolbar, sorting, pagination integration and mobile filter panel were added on top of it at the owner's request. E1 is therefore NOT closed.

**Deployed in this slice (all staging only, no production exists), in order:**

| Commit | Content |
|---|---|
| `1eba38a` | sidebar skin + duplicate-UI cleanup + Show More count reuse (entry 2026-11-11 below) |
| `6dd6a8a` | pagination sink `.dp-qo-wbw-pagination-sink` (plugin template, owner-approved exception) + sticky sidebar (entry below) |
| `15251bd` | results toolbar: `.dp-qo-pagination` moved into `.dp-qo-toolbar` (left), WBW sort (right) with a visible "Poredaj po" label, WBW English "Sort by" title row hidden; plugin template + `quick-order-filters.scss/.css` |
| `5ea11f8` | sort select wrapped by the theme Select2 (`select2-init.js`, `dreampoint_b2b_needs_select2()` + admin-bar panel offset in `functions.php`); mobile off-canvas filter panel reusing the Shop/Brands mechanism (class contract `products-lhs-filter-wrap` / `close-mobile-filters` / `.sort-filter .filter-btn` + `mobile-product-filters.js`, plugin template); toolbar spacing 24px above / 20px below; shared SCSS mixins `mobile-filter-panel`, `mobile-filter-close`, `sort-select2-chip`, `sort-select2-panel` in `_mixins.scss` (Shop's `shop-archive.css` compiled output byte-identical); initial sort selection matches ProductList (`title` asc unless a valid `orderby` URL param) |
| `177afa5` | open Select2 dropdown skin: `body:has(.dp-quick-order)` added to the shared `:is(...)` panel rule in `_content.scss` (compiled into `style.css`, 14 occurrences, otherwise byte-identical); Quick Order only auto-close of the mobile panel after a trusted checkbox / radio `change` (`mobile-product-filters.js`, gated on `.dp-quick-order`, Shop untouched); script versioned by mtime in the Quick Order enqueue block (`functions.php`) |

**Root causes recorded for future work:**
- *Dropdown styling:* the shared Select2 panel skin in `_content.scss` is scoped by a list of body classes (account, contact, product archives); Quick Order has no identifying body class, so its panel fell back to the default Select2 look.
- *Sort state:* WBW builds a per-view URL, so filtering through filter 3 drops `orderby` from the URL and WBW's own code resets the sort select (view 2) to its first option (also without Select2), while ProductList (`#applyOrderbyParam`) keeps the last sort when `orderby` is absent. `select2-init.js` remembers the user's pick (`select2:select`) and restores the select value + Select2 display on `wpfAjaxSuccess` without triggering `change` (no extra WBW request). A read-only review verified this (event order reset -> restore, no duplicate handlers, WBW reloads on `popstate` outside Safari). A pre-existing defect was fixed in the same file: the control displayed `price` while the list was sorted by `title` asc by default.
- *Mobile panel:* the Shop archive does not close the panel in JS after a pick; it appears to close only because WBW reloads the whole page there. Quick Order filters via AJAX (no reload), so the panel needed an explicit, Quick Order-gated close.

**Verification levels (distinct):**
- Implementation + local QA (visible local browser, real clicks): PASS for every item above (desktop 1440, mobile 390 / 800, pagination, sort persistence across pagination / filter / search / reset, Select2 lifecycle after WBW AJAX, panel open / close / Escape / X / Show More / filter pick, Shop regression).
- Staging technical QA by Claude: the controlled `playwright-extension` tab stayed `visibilityState: hidden`, so only load-state facts were read (assets served, DOM, computed layout, initial sort display); real-interaction and visual checks on staging were NOT VERIFIED by Claude.
- Owner acceptance: manual review on staging by the owner (2026-11-12) covers the sub-scope listed above.

**Accepted limitations / still unverified:** no focus trap or focus return in the mobile panel (shared Shop JS, unchanged); the sort control can disagree with the URL only in Safari after back / forward (WBW does not reload there; same as before); the URL loses `orderby` after a WBW filter pick (WBW behaviour, results keep the sort); B2B restricted-user (`vis_*`) perspective of the filter vocabulary and ADR-015 term scope on staging, physical mobile device and Croatian locale of the remaining WBW strings are not recorded as verified; English WBW strings seen locally (In Stock, Out of Stock, Search ..., Clear, Show More) are tracked as B3 (COMPLETE on staging 2026-11-02) / X1 and were not re-verified on staging in this slice. The `functions.php` editor diagnostic "`$attachment` unused" (line ~795) is older and unrelated.

**E1 remaining (updated 2026-11-12: chips and the bottom cart bar are DONE and OWNER VERIFIED, see the E1 slice 1 entry above; the rest is NOT STARTED, own plugin `dp-b2b-quick-order`, plugin approval needed per task):** page header / title / intro (the plugin has its own uppercase "QUICK ORDER" header and no intro) with Search / Excel Import positioning, and typography of the product list / rows / column labels / controls. These are the `T10` items of `figma-alignment.md` section 42 (estimate 4 h for header / typography / chips; rest UNKNOWN). Already done under other IDs and not part of the remaining scope: QO-03 empty state (E4 part A, plugin 1.0.38, `6d3422c`), Excel import modals QO-04..07 (ADR-017, X11), Quick Order functional baseline v1.0.36.

## 2026-11-12 (E1 Quick Order: pagination sink + sticky sidebar) - DEPLOYED TO STAGING; PARTLY VERIFIED (scroll behaviour NOT VERIFIED); E1 STILL OPEN

> **Update (2026-11-12, later):** sticky sidebar and Show More / Show Fewer were manually accepted by the owner (see the entry above). The "NOT VERIFIED" lines below describe the Claude staging QA at that time and are kept as history.

Commit `6dd6a8a2ea6cd1c9f6a14581967437c42bd3477c`, pushed to `origin/master` (no force) and deployed with `git pull --ff-only` as site user `dream9399` (staging tree clean before and after, ancestry `1eba38a` -> `6dd6a8a` verified, files owned by `dream9399`). Deployed files (exactly three): `plugins/dp-b2b-quick-order/templates/quick-order.php`, `themes/dreampoint-b2b/sass/pages/quick-order-filters.scss`, `themes/dreampoint-b2b/css/pages/quick-order-filters.css`. The pull also brought the earlier docs-only commit `8d9355d`. PHP lint passed; compiled CSS verified byte-identical to a fresh SCSS build. No WBW configuration change, no cache purge, no production access.

- **Bug (owner report):** after search `urban` + a WBW filter + "Poništi filtere", a native `1 2 3 4 ... 38 ->` pagination bar appeared above the search field.
- **Root cause (reproduced locally):** the hidden `.dp-qo-wbw-ajax-placeholder` is WBW's configured Product List target, but when no `.woocommerce-pagination` exists in the container WBW inserts its response pagination as a SIBLING after the loop container (`frontend.woofilters.js`, `loopContainer.eq(0).after(newPagination)`), i.e. outside the hidden element and at the top of `.dp-quick-order`. It appeared on every filter update with a multi-page result (not only on reset); single-page results returned empty pagination HTML, so nothing was inserted. Not CSS, not a plugin-JS move. A first attempt (nav nested inside the placeholder) failed because WBW overwrites the placeholder content with `.html()` on every update.
- **Fix:** a separate hidden wrapper `.dp-qo-wbw-pagination-sink` holding an empty `nav.woocommerce-pagination`, right after the placeholder. WBW finds it and replaces it in place, inside the hidden wrapper. No JS, no CSS hiding. Review after WBW updates (relies on WBW's replace-existing-nav behaviour).
- **Sticky sidebar:** reuses the Shop/brand `.products-lhs-filter-wrap` rule (`shop-archive.scss`): `@include bp(md-up)`, `position: sticky; top: 96px; align-self: flex-start`, `128px` with `body.admin-bar`, scoped to `.dp-quick-order .row > .col-lg-3`. Pure CSS; no ancestor with overflow/contain/transform blocks sticky. Below 992px the column stacks and is not sticky (as on Shop; Quick Order has no mobile drawer).
- **Local QA (PASS):** original sequence leaves 0 stray WooCommerce pagination and exactly one `.dp-qo-pagination`; next-page navigation, single-page result, search cleared, second filter and reset all correct; sticky holds at `top: 128px` (admin bar) while scrolling and releases at the end of the row before the footer; 390px and 800px not sticky, no horizontal overflow; console clean.
- **Staging QA (authenticated `playwright-extension` session, admin; the controlled tab stayed `visibilityState: hidden`, so real clicks timed out and all interaction below used SYNTHETIC events - `element.click()` / `input` event - and no screenshots; scroll offsets are not applied in a hidden tab):**
  - VERIFIED: staging HEAD `6dd6a8a`, tree clean; deployed CSS served HTTP 200 with the sticky rules; template contains the sink.
  - VERIFIED (synthetic): original sequence search `urban` -> WBW Brand filter -> "Poništi filtere" (search kept `urban`): 0 stray `.woocommerce-pagination` outside the sink after every step, exactly one `.dp-qo-pagination`, "Strana 1 / 1" single-page rows unchanged; filter with a multi-page WBW result and reset: WBW fills the sink in place (4 then 9 page links, wrapper `display: none`), stray 0, Quick Order pagination back to "Strana 1 / 10 Sljedeća ->"; console 0 errors. Note `urban` is a single-page result on staging, so the sink was exercised through the multi-page filter/reset case.
  - VERIFIED (computed style, not scroll): Quick Order column `position: sticky; top: 128px; align-self: flex-start` (admin bar present) in 1440x900 and 1440x1080 iframes, no ancestor with overflow/contain/transform, no horizontal overflow; at 390px `position: relative; top: 0`, no horizontal overflow; Shop `.products-lhs-filter-wrap` still `sticky; top: 128px; align-self: flex-start`. Column height on staging in the default collapsed state is 570px (fits both viewports).
  - **NOT VERIFIED:** actual scroll behaviour (stays below the header while scrolling, releases at the end of the row before the footer, lower controls reachable) - needs a visible foreground browser window; tall-sidebar behaviour with expanded groups; Show More / Fewer (6 "Prikaži više" controls found, the synthetic click did not expand them, inconclusive); real pointer / touch; physical mobile.
- **Limitation (tall sidebar):** with all groups expanded the column is about 1102px high (900px viewport), so while it is stuck its lowest ~330px are below the fold and become reachable only by scrolling to the end of the row. Same accepted behaviour as Shop (sections can be collapsed); a CSS-only fix without nested scrolling does not exist, so none was added. To be judged on staging at 900 and 1080px viewport heights.
- **Owner actions remaining (superseded by the 2026-11-12 acceptance entry above):** bring the controlled Chrome window to the foreground (or check by hand) for the scroll / tall-sidebar / Show More checks above, then explicit visual acceptance. E1 stays OPEN (whole page); header / intro / list / chips remain NOT STARTED.

## 2026-11-11 (P1 incident: WBW parent category returns no products) - OWNER VERIFIED - CLOSED (2026-11-11)

Separate functional incident (not B2 / T9, not C1-1). Reported on staging: `/shop/?wpf_fbv=1&product_brand_list=izipizi&wpf_filter_cat_list_1s=naocale` shows no products.

- **Data (staging, read-only, admin context):** brand Izipizi 72 published products; category Naocale 53 products, 0 directly assigned (all in sub-categories Sunčane / Za čitanje / Zaštita od ekrana / Dodaci / Sportske / Sleeping); Izipizi AND Naocale incl. descendants = 53, all catalog-visible and purchasable. Every top-level category except the demo one has 0 direct products, so selecting ANY parent in the WBW sidebar returned 0.
- **Root cause (WBW 3.4.8, identical vendor file on local and staging):** `addCustomTaxQuery()` builds the `product_cat` clause of every `wpf_filter_cat_list*` key with `include_children => false` (keys without `_list` use true). The Multi / flat sidebar always emits the `_list` form. "Filter with children" (`f_extend_parent_filtering`) is ignored for the Multi type (`findTaxonomyIncludeChildrenStatus()`). Not related to the B2 / T9 brand layer (`wpf_` keys already put WBW in `url` mode) nor to C1-1.
- **Dashboard-only option rejected by the owner:** `Show hierarchical` + `Extend parent select` fixes sidebar clicks but changes the approved flat B2 / T9 sidebar (nested tree, Show More inside sub-lists, 15 chips for one parent, long URLs) and does not fix direct URLs with only the parent slug. Local pilot restored byte-for-byte afterwards (md5 of `wp_wpf_filters` id 1 verified).
- **Query lifecycle (temporary local diagnostic, removed):** WBW writes its clause inside `woocommerce_product_query` (WBW priority 999), which WooCommerce runs from `pre_get_posts` priority 10. From `pre_get_posts` 11 through `parse_tax_query` the main query's `tax_query` holds WBW's group `[ {taxonomy product_cat, field slug|term_id, terms, operator IN|AND|NOT IN, include_children false}, 'wpf_group' => 'product_cat', 'relation' => ... ]`, and WBW sets the query var `wpf_query = 1`. The executed SQL contained only the parent term_taxonomy_id (`IN (236)`); after the fix it contains the parent plus 17 descendants and the result went from 0 to 9. Quick Order's own guard (`dp-b2b-quick-order/inc/class-filter-bridge.php`) clears `wpf_query` on its queries at priority 10000.
- **Fix:** new `inc/wbw-category-children-compat.php` (required next to the other WBW compat files in `functions.php`), `pre_get_posts` priority 10001. Only on non-admin queries that carry `wpf_query` and a `wpf_filter_cat_list*` request key, and only inside WBW's `product_cat` `wpf_group`, clauses with `include_children = false` are corrected: IN -> `include_children true`; NOT IN -> `include_children true` (exclusion of a parent excludes its sub-tree; decision point, see limits); AND with 2+ terms -> nested AND group with one single-term IN clause per category, each `include_children true` (core would otherwise expand the terms to parent + all descendants and require all of them). Terms, field, group structure and other clauses untouched; idempotent; native WP tax-query child expansion, no manual descendant lookups; no URL, UI, WBW setting or vendor change. Removal: delete the file and its `require` once WBW supports descendants for `wpf_filter_cat_list*`.
- **Local QA (localhost, real frontend requests, independent WP_Query expectations; 19/19 PASS, no duplicates):** parent 0 -> 214; parent + brand 0 -> 9; parent + stock 0 -> 160; parent + brand + stock 9; OR `lifestyle|toys` 0 -> 281; AND `dev-elektronika,dev-baterije-i-napajanje` 0 -> 21 (AND `edukativne-igracke,puzzle` 1 unchanged, AND of disjoint trees 0); parent + leaf in parent 29 -> 71; parent with direct + descendant products 8 -> 65; NOT parent 427 -> 213, NOT parent + brand 4, NOT `dev-elektronika` 419 -> 356; term-id variant `wpf_filter_cat_list_1=236` 214; leaf only 21 and brand only 13 unchanged; unfiltered 427; native archive 214; archive + WBW leaf / brand archive + parent correct. Flat sidebar interaction (click parent, brand, In Stock, sort, chip x, Clear All, Back / Forward) correct and consistent; console clean; no PHP errors from the new file; parent request ~+50-150 ms versus leaf (longer IN list).
- **Visual regression:** list stays flat (`data-show-hierarchical=false`), one chip per selection, Show More (31) / Show Fewer unchanged, sticky wrapper and mobile off-canvas (fixed, scrollable, body locked) unchanged, no horizontal overflow. No SCSS / CSS touched.
- **Limits / NOT verified:** WBW's AJAX refresh endpoint (admin-ajax) builds its own query and is not covered (the sidebar navigates, so it is not the active path locally; unverified on staging); a WBW preselect category clause on a request that also carries the URL key would be corrected too (none configured); NOT IN broadening is a decision (switchable by dropping the NOT IN case); staging NOT verified (needs an authenticated browser: expected 53 for the reported URL in the admin visibility context, account-specific visibility may reduce it).
- **Owner decisions (2026-11-11):** flat B2 / T9 sidebar kept, WBW dashboard settings unchanged; descendant-inclusive behavior approved for IN, AND and NOT IN (excluding a parent also excludes its descendants).
- **Closure (2026-11-11):** implementation commit `314285bd1302f7833a9357bcb7d1270e12bd3644` (`inc/wbw-category-children-compat.php`, one `require` in `functions.php`, this entry), local regression 19/19 PASS, controlled staging deployment as a fast-forward (`19fc57b` -> `314285b`), staging server-side verification (reported combination 0 -> 53, parent 0 -> 53, leaf 16, brand 72, unfiltered 451, `NOT` parent 451 -> 398, native archive 53; WBW filter 1 settings md5 unchanged, no PHP errors; a WP-CLI simulation, not a browser test), then the owner confirmed ALL FIVE authenticated staging browser checks on 2026-11-11: (1) the original Izipizi + Naocale URL returns the matching products, (2) selecting the Naocale parent in the sidebar includes descendant categories, (3) combined brand + stock + sorting works, (4) chip removal and Clear All work, (5) the flat B2 / T9 sidebar, Show More / Show Fewer and the console are unchanged.
- **Decisions kept:** flat B2 / T9 sidebar and unchanged WBW dashboard settings; descendant-inclusive for IN, AND and NOT IN (excluding a parent excludes its descendants).
- **Maintenance:** review the compatibility layer after every WBW (woo-product-filter / woofilter-pro) update; delete `inc/wbw-category-children-compat.php` and its `require` once WBW itself includes descendants for `wpf_filter_cat_list*`. Not covered by design: WBW's admin-ajax refresh path (not active for the archive).
- **Boundaries:** B2 / T9 stays closed; C1-1 sorting stays deployed and functionally owner-verified, C1 itself stays open (C1-2 pagination NOT STARTED, C1-3 subtitle DEFERRED); E1 Quick Order filter styling NOT STARTED; removal of the average-rating sort option is a separate pending task.

## 2026-11-11 (C1-1 archive sort bar) - IMPLEMENTED AND LOCALLY QA-CHECKED, NOT COMMITTED; AWAITING OWNER APPROVAL FOR COMMIT / STAGING; C1 STAYS OPEN [historical: committed `19fc57b`, deployed and owner verified; C1 later COMPLETE, see 2026-11-13 C1 closure]

Scope: Figma CAT-02 (`11148:33646`) sort bar node `11149:63768` on Shop, product category and brand archives. Not in this slice: C1-2 pagination, C1-3 archive subtitle, E1, B2 / T9 sidebar, product cards, global Select2.

- **Files:** `woocommerce/loop/orderby.php` (new override of core 9.7.0: always-rendered visible label `Sortiraj po:` bound to the select by `for`/`id`, replaces the core `aria-label`; everything else identical to core), `sass/pages/shop-archive.scss` (+ compiled `css/pages/shop-archive.css`), `sass/components/_content.scss` (+ compiled `style.css`: the archive body classes `post-type-archive-product`, `tax-product_cat`, `tax-product_brand` were added to the existing account/contact Select2 panel rule, so the open panel reuses that skin; no other page is affected).
- **Closed state (measured, 1440):** bar 28px high, 16px to the product column (was 62px / 20px; an empty `.woocommerce-notices-wrapper` no longer wraps the flex row); label 12/400 `#6c727e`, 11px gap; chip `#f3f4f6`, radius 4, 28px high, value 12/600, letter-spacing 0.24px, `#0d121c`, 16px chevron (`\e91a`, the theme-select glyph; the old `\e902` rule never won the cascade against `#page .select2-container`, so the previous arrow was already `\e91a`); result count 12/400 `#6c727e` with 16px inset, dynamic text unchanged. Chip width follows the selected label (133px for "Default sorting", Figma 96px is for "Najnovije").
- **Open state (Figma has none):** shared account/contact panel skin (14px options, 8x12 padding, `#f3f4f6` selected/highlighted, 6px radius), z-index 10000, all six options visible without an inner scrollbar. The panel opens at the chip's left edge, so it may extend up to ~66px past the column to the right; it stayed inside the viewport at 390/768/992/1100/1440. At 992 the panel narrows (156px) and long labels wrap to two lines.
- **Mobile (<= md):** the form is a full-width row (label + chip, 36px high touch target); result count stays hidden; the `Filtriraj` button keeps its own row below. Figma is desktop-only.
- **Select2 click interception:** NOT a user-facing defect. At scroll offsets 0 and 250 the trigger is the hit target (desktop and 390); it is only covered once it has scrolled behind the sticky header, which is expected. The earlier timeout was Playwright scrolling the element to the top under the sticky header. No code change.
- **Translations:** the local admin is `en_US` and no `woocommerce-hr` language pack exists locally (no `wp-content/languages`, none in the theme), so the Croatian sort labels could not be evaluated locally. Nothing changed; verify on staging as an `hr` user. Default sort order untouched. The new `Sortiraj po:` msgid lives in the `dreampoint-b2b` domain (same pattern as the result count).
- **QA (localhost, real pointer clicks, no persistent data written):** Shop, category (Toys) and brand (AlphaGrade) sorted by real click (price low-high, price high-low, latest); active WBW filter preserved when sorting; 8 results descending, count "8 proizvoda" matches; keyboard Enter / ArrowDown / Enter works, focus ring visible; no horizontal overflow at 390/768/992/1100/1440.
- **WBW SORT FAILURE - ROOT CAUSE CONFIRMED AND FIXED IN THEME (pre-existing, not caused by C1-1):** on an archive URL WITHOUT a query string (e.g. `/product-category/toys/`) choosing a sort option did nothing. Cause: the `data-filter-settings` attribute of `.wpfMainWrapper` (filter id 1) is invalid JSON (`"order":"[{"id"...`, unescaped quotes at char ~4981), the same cause as ADR-015 / `js/wbw-filter-param-fix.js`. WBW's `changeOrderBy()` (`frontend.woofilters.js` ~173-175, 3.4.8) reads `$wrapper.data('filter-settings')`; jQuery cannot parse it and returns the raw string, `filter.settings` is undefined, and `filter.settings.filters` throws `TypeError: Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'filters')` before `location.href` is assigned. The handler `return false`s, so WooCommerce's own form submit never runs either. With a `?` in the URL WBW takes an else branch that never reads the settings, so sorting worked there. WBW's own tolerant parser `getFilterMainSettings()` (fallback `}]"` / `"[{` replacement) parses the attribute correctly; only this one call site bypasses it. It affects every archive that renders `[wpf-filters id=1]` (Shop, category, brand, search), not Quick Order. Even with valid settings WBW would have appended a dangling `&` (empty `filters.defaults`).
- **Fix (theme only, no WBW file touched):** `js/wbw-filter-param-fix.js` (already enqueued on shop / product taxonomy / search only, deferred) now also seeds jQuery's data cache for each `.wpfMainWrapper` whose `.data('filter-settings')` is still a string with the object from `getFilterMainSettings()` (empty `filters.defaults` dropped from that copy), at init and again after any `ajaxComplete` (idempotent). The attribute, WBW's handler and WooCommerce sorting are unchanged; no new event handler. Same removal criterion as the existing fix: delete when a WBW update fixes the lookup. Also `woocommerce/loop/orderby.php`: `autocomplete="off"` on the select, so back/forward no longer restores a stale native value while Select2 shows the label of the URL (observed before: label "high to low", select value `date`).
- **Local QA of the fix (real pointer clicks, product order verified, console clean):** category (no query): price low-high ascending, price high-low descending, latest = post_date descending (checked with WP-CLI `post list`), repeated changes, back/forward keep label and native value in sync, one navigation per change (no duplicate history entries); Shop (no query) low-high, and `/shop/page/3/` ascending; brand (no query) high-low descending, keyboard Enter / ArrowDown / Enter on a clean URL works; unrelated parameter (`utm_source`) + category filter preserved (`...&orderby=price`, no trailing `&`); brand-only filter (`wpf_fbv=1&product_brand_list=`) 13 results descending; two filters (category + brand) 2 results ascending, both chips kept, chip x removal keeps `orderby`; Clear All keeps `orderby` and unrelated params; Quick Order page loads without errors and does not load the fix script (no `.woocommerce-ordering` there). C1-1 visuals re-checked after the fix: bar 28px, label and chip unchanged, count 12/400, panel, 768 and 390 layouts, mobile `Filtriraj` opens the filter panel (`filter-open`), no horizontal overflow.
- **Staging (read-only, NOT reproduced):** `frontend.woofilters.js` on staging has the identical md5 (`d1685267...`), WBW 3.4.8, and the same `.data('filter-settings')` line 174. The staging bug itself is NOT confirmed: guests are redirected to `/my-account`, no authenticated browser session was available, and whether filter 1's settings serialize to the same invalid JSON there was not checked. Verify on staging after deploy (sort on a clean `/shop/`, category and brand URL).
- **Remaining limitations (not fixed, WBW behavior):** WBW's sort handler keeps `/page/N/` in the URL, so changing the sort on `/shop/page/3/` shows page 3 of the new order (native WooCommerce would reset to page 1). Seeding is skipped when jQuery already parses the attribute. Croatian sort labels still unverified (see above).
- **Status:** C1-1 plus the WBW compatibility fix are implemented and locally verified, NOT committed, awaiting owner approval for commit and staging; C1 stays open (C1-2 pagination and C1-3 subtitle pending).

## 2026-11-11 (B2 / T9 CLOSURE) - OWNER VERIFIED - CLOSED (2026-11-11)

The owner completed the final authenticated staging verification and approved closing B2 / T9. Final owner results: Show More / Show Fewer PASS; price slider hover and keyboard focus PASS; every earlier B2 / T9 staging issue confirmed resolved or explicitly accepted. Last deployed implementation commit `42cf2062d9900dcbb4bf0309d6cb75baad4c24fc` (local = origin/master = staging at that point). The 2026-11-11 and 2026-11-10 entries below stay as history; where they say "awaiting owner verification" this entry supersedes them.
- **Verified final outcomes:** (1) WBW archive filter styling aligned with Figma CAT-02 (`11148:33646`) on `/shop/`, category and brand archives. (2) Brand-only filtering compatibility fix implemented and deployed (`inc/wbw-brand-filter-compat.php`). (3) Categories and Brands Show More / Show Fewer enabled and working. (4) Croatian labels and dynamic hidden-option counts verified by the owner. (5) Mobile off-canvas filter panel scrolls to the bottom controls. (6) Price slider alignment, hover (brand border, white fill) and keyboard focus verified. (7) Active-filter chips positioned above the card, with the 24px brand-hero spacing. (8) Desktop sticky filter sidebar implemented and accepted. (9) Existing Clear / Ocisti behaviour retained (no duplicate Clear All in the chips row). (10) Quick Order functionality and styling preserved (theme overrides are scoped under `.products-lhs-filter`).
- **Technical decisions to keep:** the WBW 3.4.8 brand-filter compatibility layer stays necessary until the vendor's `isFiltered()` recognises `product_brand_list` (re-test `/shop/?product_brand_list=<slug>&wpf_fbv=1` without the file after a WBW update, then delete it). WBW staging filter 1 has `f_max_show_more = "5"` on the Categories and Brands blocks (set 2026-11-11, backup `/home/dreampoint.b2b/backup-recovery/wbw-filter1-showmore-20261111-085847/`); the owner manually enabled the WBW option `display_view_more` and entered the Croatian labels "Prikazi vise" / "Prikazi manje" (Show More was invisible until that option was on - closed, do not re-investigate). The sticky sidebar is intentionally allowed to be taller than the viewport; the owner accepted that lower controls may need section collapse, that the chips row passes under the sticky header while stuck, and the other limitations recorded in the 2026-11-11 entry (real iOS Safari not tested).
- **Still open and independent (not part of B2 / T9):** C1 archive alignment is NOT complete: the Shop sorting dropdown must be styled like the theme's other select controls, and the sort bar / pagination / other archive parts remain unaudited. E1 (Quick Order visual filter styling) is a separate task. All other roadmap items keep their own status.

## 2026-11-11 (B2 / T9 owner QA corrections after authenticated staging review) - IMPLEMENTED AND LOCALLY QA-CHECKED, NOT COMMITTED; AWAITING OWNER APPROVAL FOR COMMIT/DEPLOY; CLOSED LATER THE SAME DAY (see the closure entry above)

Staging HEAD `a243b40`, local HEAD `4eae069` (docs only). Local working tree only: no commit, push, deploy, WBW vendor change or database/WBW-setting change.
- **1. Brand filter returns "no products" (P1) - ROOT CAUSE CONFIRMED, FIXED IN THEME.** `/shop/?wpf_fbv=1&product_brand_list=<slug>` (what WBW itself writes when a brand checkbox is ticked) built a tax query `product_brand`, `field => id`, `terms => [<slug>]`; WP casts the slug to 0 and the SQL becomes `0 = 1` (logged from the real request). Cause in WBW 3.4.8: `getQueryVars()` selects the tax-query mode from `isFiltered()`, which only recognises `orderby`, `wpf_*` (except `wpf_count/wpf_fbv/wpf_dpv/wpf_skip`), `product_tag*` and `pr_*`; `product_brand_list` is none of them, so a brand-only URL runs in mode `default` (term IDs) instead of `url` (slugs). Any extra recognised key (category, stock, attribute, `orderby`, `wpf_order`) flips it back and the brand works - which is why combined filters worked. NOT an index problem: `wpf_meta_keys` has no brand key, so `wpf_fbv` variation/index logic is not involved; the brand archive uses a different query path. Evidence: local reproduction through the real UI click (`dev-bestforce`: empty before, 15 products after); the four WBW/Pro files that matter (`class-woobewoo-pf-woofilters.php`, `woo-product-filter.php`, `class-woobewoo-pf-woofilterpro.php`, `frontend.woofilters.js`) are byte-identical (md5) on staging and localhost; staging brand `24bottles` has 28 published products (read-only WP-CLI). Staging was not exercised in a browser (no staging login in this session): confidence HIGH for the same mechanism, but the fix is verified locally only. Fix: new `inc/wbw-brand-filter-compat.php` (required from `functions.php`) adds the neutral marker `wpf_dp_brand=1` to the in-memory `$_GET` only when `product_brand_list` is present and WBW would not otherwise see a filter; no URL change, no vendor file. Safety review (2026-11-11): the hook runs on `parse_request` (priority 1: before the main query and WBW's `pre_get_posts` handling, query vars known), only on the frontend and only for product archives (shop post-type archive, product taxonomies), so admin, admin-ajax, REST, WP-CLI (no `$_GET`) and the Quick Order page never match; the value must be a string of slugs separated by `,` or `|` (arrays and injection-like values are ignored); an existing `wpf_dp_brand` is not overwritten; nothing is persisted. Re-tested after the move: single (15), multi (30), brand + stock (13), category archive (12), no-param shop (427) and the Quick Order URL unaffected. Pre-existing vendor behaviour, unrelated: `product_brand_list[]=x` (array) makes WBW return HTTP 500. Verified locally: single brand, comma-separated multi-brand (30 = 15 + 15), brand + stock, brand on a category archive (`lifestyle`: 12 products), chip removal, Clear. Remove the file when WBW recognises `product_brand_list` in `isFiltered()`. Note: WBW's brand multi-select delimiter is `,` (OR) and `|` (AND) - the reverse of tags.
- **2. Show More missing on staging (P1) - ROOT CAUSE CONFIRMED; the owner approved the WBW setting change on 2026-11-11 and it was APPLIED ON STAGING the same day (see the deployment paragraph below).** The control is created client-side by WBW only when the block has `f_max_show_more`. Localhost filter 1: Category and Brand blocks have `f_max_show_more = 5` (control present and clipped nowhere: ul max-height 200px holds 5 rows + the control, `Prikazi vise (N)` works with real counts, Show Fewer returns). Staging filter 1: the Category and Brand blocks have NO `f_max_show_more` (only `f_max_height = 200`), so WBW renders no control at all - a WBW configuration drift between local and staging (DB content is not synced by git). Theme CSS/JS and the PO/MO are not the cause. Remediation (pending approval, same method as the 2026-11-10 `f_enable_title_mobile` change: WBW model `updateById`, dry run, sha256 + backup of `wp_wpf_filters`): set `f_max_show_more = "5"` on the Category and Brand blocks of staging filter 1. Attribute blocks have no `f_max_show_more` on localhost either (their "Show More" comes from a different, scroll-based WBW path). Also seen: staging block order differs (Brand first, Price last) and staging attribute blocks have empty `f_list` - not touched.
- **3. Mobile off-canvas scrolling (P1) - FIXED.** `.products-lhs-filter-wrap` (fixed panel, < 992px) now is the single scroll container (`overflow-y: auto`, `overscroll-behavior: contain`, `100dvh`, 32px bottom padding); the close button is `position: fixed` (20px from the top, below the admin bar when shown, white round background); `body.filter-open { overflow: hidden }` below 992px only. Verified at 390x600 and 768x420: content 1379px scrolls in a 600/420px panel, the Filter/Clear buttons end fully visible at the bottom, close button always reachable, section collapse/expand still changes the height, no horizontal overflow, body lock released after close. Not verified: real iOS Safari (address bar behaviour), screen reader.
- **4. Price filter alignment - FIXED (owner CSS refined by measurement).** Original: track 31px / 15px from the card edge (WBW 100%-wide slider + 8px margins overflowed the 10px padded box). Owner's proposal (`padding 0 / 15px`) gave track 21 / 20 but number inputs 13 / 28. Final: content padding 0 and slider `width: auto`: track 21 / 21, handles 13 / 13, inputs 13 / 13 (flush with titles and rows). Slider config, keyboard step (ArrowRight moves the minimum and updates the URL; verified with dispatched key events), focus ring and editable inputs unchanged.
- **5. Brand archive chips/hero spacing - FIXED.** Chips hung flush under the hero (gap 0). `body.tax-product_brand` with active chips: row margin-top 36px (12 + `$dp-space-24`) -> 24px hero-to-chips, 48px chips-to-card kept; no filters: unchanged. Shop and category archives keep the 12px rule.
- **6. Sticky desktop sidebar - OWNER APPROVED, IMPLEMENTED (supersedes "sticky deliberately rejected").** `.products-lhs-filter-wrap` at >= 992px: `position: sticky; top: 96px` (72px sticky header + 24px gap; 128px with the admin bar), `align-self: flex-start`; CSS only. Verified at 992 / 1100 / 1440: sticks at 128px (24px under the header), ends with the row (64px above the footer), no overlap. Known consequences: the 1255px card is taller than the viewport, so its lower part (Filter/Clear buttons) is reachable by collapsing sections or at the end of the list; the active-chips row hangs 48px above the card and so travels with it and passes under the header (z-index 9998) while stuck, visible again at the top of the page.
- **7. Clear All** stays absent from the chips row (owner accepted); the card's Clear button is the reset (verified).
- **8. Archive sorting dropdown styling** = outstanding C1 item, NOT done here (see master-roadmap C1).
- **Files:** `inc/wbw-brand-filter-compat.php` (new), `functions.php` (+1 require), `sass/pages/shop-archive.scss`, `css/pages/shop-archive.css` (generated). Regression: Quick Order unchanged ("+ Show More", no theme sidebar, no sticky); 0 console errors; no horizontal overflow at 390/768/992/1100/1440.
- **Deployment (2026-11-11):** committed `3af8964` and pushed; staging working tree was clean, `git pull --ff-only` took staging from `a243b40` to `3af8964` (= origin/master); `php -l` passed on `inc/wbw-brand-filter-compat.php` and `functions.php`; the deployed `css/pages/shop-archive.css` contains the sticky and brand-archive rules; no root-owned files in the theme. Staging only, no production exists.
- **WBW staging configuration (2026-11-11, owner-authorised):** filter 1, blocks wpfBrand (order index 0 on staging) and wpfCategory (index 1): `f_max_show_more` absent -> `"5"`. Done through WBW's model `updateById(['settings' => ...])` after a dry run (exactly 2 changed setting keys, stored JSON reproduced byte for byte, block order unchanged). `setting_data` sha256 `5d013a29...c486` (28500 bytes) -> `0496896a...138b` (28544 bytes); read back equal to the expected blob; `meta_keys` (empty) and title unchanged; filters 2 and 3 byte-identical (sha256 before/after). Backup outside the webroot (mode 700, site user, raw blob + all rows + restore note): `/home/dreampoint.b2b/backup-recovery/wbw-filter1-showmore-20261111-085847/`. A server-side render of `[wpf-filters id=1]` as an administrator shows `data-max-showmore="5"` on the Category and Brand blocks. The visible control is created by WBW's JavaScript in the browser: NOT yet seen on staging (no authenticated browser access in this session), nor the Croatian labels with counts.
- **Tooling lessons (WBW + WP-CLI):** (1) WBW does not bootstrap under WP-CLI because `woobewoo_pf_request()` needs a `REQUEST_URI`; pass `wp --exec='$_SERVER["REQUEST_URI"]="/";'` before using `WooBeWoo_PF_Frame`. (2) `WooBeWoo_PF_Woofilters_Model::updateById()` rebuilds `setting_data` from `$data['settings']` (and `meta_keys` from the filter order): passing a raw `setting_data` key replaces the filter with an empty stub (reproduced on a local filter, restored from backup, no staging impact); pass the full `['settings' => $unserialized['settings']]` array and verify a no-op write is byte-identical first. (3) Do not run the apply on staging before a local lossless-write test.
- **Staging verification status:** server-side only (deployed files, syntax, CSS rules, WBW attribute render, config read-back). NOT verified on staging in a browser: brand-only filtering for 24Bottles, Show More / Show Fewer control and Croatian labels, mobile panel scrolling on a real phone, sticky sidebar, price alignment. Owner steps: open `/shop/` as an approved user, tick 24Bottles (expect 28 products and a chip), check Show More (N) on Categories and Brands, price slider alignment, sticky sidebar at laptop width, phone filter panel scrolling to Clear/Filter, brand archive chips spacing.
- **Known limitations:** see items 3 and 6 above (real iOS Safari not tested; lower filter controls need section collapse while the sidebar is stuck; chips row passes under the header when stuck). B2 / T9 stays IMPLEMENTED - AWAITING OWNER VERIFICATION.
- **Owner staging verification (2026-11-11, later the same day):** the owner completed an authenticated staging review: brand filtering, mobile scrolling, sticky sidebar, price alignment and brand chip spacing are resolved or accepted, and the owner-accepted limitations above stand. The missing Show More control had a second cause besides the block setting: the WBW general option `display_view_more` was disabled; the owner enabled it manually on staging (no Claude change), the native control now appears, and `f_max_show_more = 5` stays in place. Show More visibility is therefore closed; do not re-investigate.
- **Final visual corrections (owner-approved, committed and deployed to staging after the owner approval, see `git log` for the hash; still needs verification in a browser on staging):** (1) Show More / Show Fewer restyled to Figma CAT-02 "Sidebar button" without checkbox (`11148:33717`, text `11148:33718`: Montserrat Medium 10px / 18px, text-primary, 4px padding, 26px row, text starts at x = 4 = the checkbox column, no icon, no underline): shared rule `.wpfShowMoreWrapper, .wpfShowFewerWrapper` in `sass/pages/shop-archive.scss` (replaces the 12px/600 version); labels, count and WBW click handlers unchanged. (2) Price slider hover: the cyan came from WBW's `jquery-ui.theme.min.css` (`.ui-slider.ui-widget-content .ui-slider-handle:hover / .ui-state-hover / .ui-state-active { border-color: #4ae8ea !important }`) plus `.ui-state-hover { background: #e8eded !important }`; overridden inside the archive price filter only for `:hover`, `.ui-state-hover`, `.ui-state-active`, `.ui-state-focus` with `border-color: $dp-color-bg-brand` and `background: $dp-color-bg-primary` (`!important`, because the vendor rules are); the keyboard focus ring (`:focus-visible`) is untouched. Local QA: Show More row 26px, 10px/18px/500, text left = checkbox left, row pitch 31px, Show More (31) -> Show Fewer -> Show More (31) and the same on the mobile panel (390px), handle default / hover / active / focus classes resolve to the brand border and white fill (no cyan/grey-green), 0 console errors, no horizontal overflow, Quick Order unchanged ("+ Show More" 15.6px/700, no theme sidebar). ## 2026-11-10 (B2 / T9 final refinements: FILTERI heading, price filter, Show More count, mobile Categories) - IMPLEMENTED AND LOCALLY QA-CHECKED; COMMITTED FOR STAGING DEPLOY; AWAITING OWNER VERIFICATION (NOT CLOSED)

Follow-up to the owner-verified staging corrections (`9df4eae`, entry below, kept as history). Theme-level only; Quick Order styling stays outside B2 / T9 (task E1).
- **FILTERI heading:** theme-owned span `.dp-filter-card-title` in `header-shop-archive.php` (translatable, `esc_html_e( 'Filteri' )`), `account-card-title` typography, absolutely placed in the card's top padding (card `padding-top: 47px`, Figma: heading frame 23px at 8/8, sections at 47px); one per page, inside the card on desktop and in the off-canvas panel; the mobile close button was re-anchored (`.products-lhs-filter` became positioned) to its previous place.
- **Price filter:** 4px track (`$dp-color-border-primary`), brand-coloured range, 16px round handles with focus ring, 32px number inputs (radius 8, tokens, form focus ring), currency symbol and separator in token colours; slider keeps 8px side margins so handles never clip. `!important` only where WBW's jQuery UI theme forces it (range background); the forced 1px #000 handle border is kept. Slider drag and keyboard (ArrowRight changes the minimum and updates the URL) and editable inputs verified.
- **Filter buttons:** `.wpfFilterButtons { margin-top: 15px }` scoped to the archive sidebar.
- **Show More:** WBW Pro already creates the native control (`data-max-showmore=5` on Categories and Brands, scroll overflow on attributes); WBW has no count and prints a hardcoded "+" in English. Added the Croatian strings "Show More" -> "Prikazi vise" and "Show Fewer" -> "Prikazi manje" to `languages/plugins/woo-product-filter-hr.po/.mo` (existing entries preserved) and `js/wbw-show-more-count.js` (vanilla, archive pages only): it only rewrites the label to WBW's translated string plus the real number of `li.wpfMoreHidden` options ("Prikazi vise (31)"), drops the "+" / "-", observes `.products-lhs-filter` (childList + class attribute) and is idempotent (label cached in `data-dp-label`, no feedback loop); WBW's click handlers are unchanged (verified Show More -> Show Fewer -> Show More with recalculated count). Accepted limitation: the control is a WBW `li` that is not keyboard focusable (WBW markup, not rebuilt). The `.mo` is plugin-wide, so the Quick Order sidebar's Show More label also becomes Croatian on the `hr` locale (text only).
- **Mobile "Kategorije" (root cause):** WBW picks its mobile block settings with `wp_is_mobile()` (server-side user agent). In filter 1 the Categories block has `f_enable_title_mobile = no` (every other block `yes_close`), so on a real phone WBW prints no heading and no toggle for Categories (the same on staging; block order differs there). Playwright sends a desktop user agent, so the section was visible there. Owner-approved fix: change that one value to `yes_close` in the WBW filter configuration (applied on staging after this deploy, see the closure entry / session report). On brand archives WBW hides the Category, Brand and attribute blocks server-side (`#wpfBlock_N {display:none}`) when the archive has no matching terms (local brand 24bottles has no visible products); that is data-driven, not a defect.
- **Sticky sidebar: not implemented (owner decision).** The card is 1255px high (shop, no filters) against about 804px available at 1440x900 (900 - 72px header - 24px); a sticky sidebar would hide the lower controls and would need nested scrolling.
- **Local QA (localhost, Chrome, no orders):** 390 / 768 / 1100 / 1440 px on shop, `product-category/lifestyle/` and `/brand/24bottles/`: one heading inside the card, price controls and handles inside the card, button spacing 15px, no horizontal overflow, chips / chip removal / Clear All / checkbox keyboard unchanged, 0 console errors, Quick Order unchanged ("+ Show More", 18px WBW checkbox, no theme script).
- **Not verified:** a real mobile user agent (needs the WBW setting and a phone), Croatian labels in a browser (local locale is `en_US`; gettext checked), Safari / iOS, staging render.
- **WBW setting applied on staging (2026-11-10, owner-authorised):** filter 1 "Products filter", block `wpfCategory` (order index 1 on staging), `f_enable_title_mobile` `no` -> `yes_close`. Done through WBW's own model (`updateById`), not the admin form and not raw SQL, after a dry run: exactly one semantic change, the model reproduced the stored blob (`stripslashes` form) and `meta_keys` byte for byte; the edit replaced only that value inside the Category block of the stored order JSON. `setting_data` sha256 `98e4faf0...a9de` (28493 bytes) -> `5d013a29...c486` (28500 bytes); read back: other settings unchanged, title / `meta_keys` unchanged, block order unchanged. Backup of all `wp_wpf_filters` rows and the raw filter 1 `setting_data` (sha verified equal to the BEFORE value) with a restore note: `/home/dreampoint.b2b/backup-recovery/wbw-filter1-mobile-title-20261110-140757/` (outside the webroot, mode 700, site user); temporary scripts removed. Check with a mobile user agent in WP-CLI (`wp_is_mobile()` true): the Categories block now renders its heading "Kategorije" and the collapsed toggle like the other blocks. The same one-time script was not needed on localhost (local data left as it is). Not verified in a real phone browser (authenticated access unavailable): awaiting owner verification.

## 2026-11-10 (B2 / T9 Figma corrections after owner review of staging `562e93a`) - IMPLEMENTED AND LOCALLY QA-CHECKED; COMMITTED FOR STAGING DEPLOY; AWAITING OWNER VERIFICATION (NOT CLOSED)

The owner reviewed the staging Shop archive after the first B2 / T9 deployment (`562e93a`, entry below, kept as history) and reported four discrepancies against Figma CAT-02 (`11148:33646`). All four were corrected as a theme-level scoped override (no WBW file, WBW setting or database change; Quick Order unchanged).
- **1. Checkboxes and 2. section spacing (measured from the Figma frame / screenshot):** header 26px (incl. the 1px rule), 8px between sections and 8px between header and list, rows 26px with a 31px pitch, checkbox 16px with 8px to the label, radius 4px, label colour `#111927`. Causes fixed: WBW's `margin-bottom: 12px` + `padding-bottom: 5px` on every block (gap was 25px), the title's inherited padding (27px high), the 5px WBW gap after the box (13px to the label) and WBW's `.wpfCheckbox.wpfMulti input { border-radius: 0 }` rule, which tied our specificity (class doubled). The unchecked border stays `#6c727e` (4.39:1) by owner decision, a deliberate accessibility deviation from the Figma `#d2d6db`.
- **3. Search field:** `.wpfSearchWrapper { display: none }` inside `.products-lhs-filter` only; it hides both WBW's own search and the fields injected by the ADR-003 compat layer (`inc/wbw-multi-search-compat.php`, untouched). Quick Order keeps its search fields.
- **4. Active-filter chips outside the card (>= 992px):** the WBW container is NOT moved in the DOM (WBW re-renders it); it is positioned above the card (`position: absolute; bottom: calc(100% + 48px)`), one row, label "Aktivni filteri" as its `::before` (text from the custom property `--dp-active-filters-label`, printed with `wp_add_inline_style` in `functions.php`, translatable), Clear All placed after the label, chips 28px, grey `#f3f4f6`. A long row scrolls horizontally instead of wrapping (wrapped rows would grow over the page title or the card because an absolutely positioned row cannot reserve its height); `max-width` follows the theme container tiers (930 / 1084 / 1200 / 1396px). While chips exist the row `#products-page .row.pos-r` gets `margin-top: 12px` (`:has`), so the card keeps Figma's 48px gap below the chips; with no filters nothing changes. Below 992px the chips stay inside the off-canvas panel. WBW's forced `margin: 5px !important` on chips, the 10px container padding, the clearfix `:after` and the `.wpfHidden` empty container were neutralised.
- **Files:** `sass/pages/shop-archive.scss`, generated `css/pages/shop-archive.css`, `functions.php` (inline custom property, +5 lines).
- **Local QA (localhost, Chrome, no orders):** at 390 / 991 / 992 / 1100 / 1440 px: section gaps 8px, titles 26px, checkbox 16px radius 4px, focus ring kept, search hidden, no horizontal overflow; >= 992px chips top flush with the title block, 48px above the card, no overlap with heading, card or product column; multiple chips and 10 cloned chips (DOM test) scroll in one row inside the container width at 992 / 1100 / 1199 / 1200 / 1300 / 1440 / 1600px; chip removal (also stock chip), Clear All, checkbox selection with Space: 0 console errors; unfiltered pages unchanged; Quick Order unaffected.
- **Remaining limitations:** Clear All (English WBW string) sits between the label and the chips (Figma has no Clear All in this row); no "FILTERI" caption and no Figma "Ocisti filtere" button at the bottom of the card; the chips row scrolls instead of wrapping; on brand archives the row starts directly under the hero (BR-02 has no chips); the horizontal scrollbar of a very long row adds up to 10px of height above the row (overlaps only the title block's bottom padding); not verified on Safari / iOS or with real AJAX re-rendering (this installation reloads the page on filter changes). No staging visual verification yet.

## 2026-11-10 (B2 / T9 WBW filter skin + C1 sidebar part) - IMPLEMENTED AND LOCALLY QA-CHECKED; COMMITTED FOR STAGING DEPLOY; AWAITING STAGING / OWNER VERIFICATION (NOT CLOSED)

Owner-approved theme-level scoped override of the WBW (woo-product-filter 3.4.8) filter sidebar on the shop, product category and brand archives, against Figma CAT-02 (`11148:33646`). Quick Order, its sidebar and filter counts are excluded (ADR-015 unchanged). No WBW plugin file, WBW admin setting or database row was changed.
- **Strategy:** `sass/pages/shop-archive.scss` block `.products-lhs-filter` (generated `css/pages/shop-archive.css`). WBW's own CSS (`frontend.woofilters.css`, `custom.woofilters.css`) loads AFTER the theme stylesheet, so every rule is scoped under `.products-lhs-filter` (3+ classes beat WBW's 2-class selectors) and uses WBW's stable class names, which survive its re-rendering. `!important` is used in two places only, both for concrete conflicts: WBW forces `#303030 !important` on checked boxes, and WBW writes an inline `background-color` on every active-filter chip.
- **Visuals:** grey `#f3f4f6` card (8px padding); section headings Montserrat 400 16px sentence case with a chevron (rotates when expanded) and a 1px rule only under an expanded section (`:has(.fa-minus)`); native checkboxes 16px, radius 2, brand fill and light tick when checked, two-tone focus ring kept; labels 14/18 `#384250`; active-filter chips 28px, radius 4, 12/600, white on the card, x icon after the label; Clear All = Figma secondary S; Filter / Clear buttons on `button-primary('m')` / `button-outline('m')`; the duplicate `.wfpDescription` line is hidden.
- **Checkbox contrast (WCAG 1.4.11):** unchecked border `#6c727e` (`$dp-color-icon-secondary`) is 4.39:1 on the `#f3f4f6` card (4.71:1 on the `#fcfcfd` fill); the Figma value `#d2d6db` was 1.33:1.
- **WBW chip-removal fix:** the x on an active-filter chip did nothing on the archives. Cause (same as ADR-015): `getFilterMainSettings()` returns `filters.order` as an array while `getFilterParam()` runs `JSON.parse` on it ("[object Object]" is not valid JSON), which aborts WBW's change handler. New `js/wbw-filter-param-fix.js` replaces only that one lookup on `window.wpfFrontendPage` with an equivalent that accepts both shapes; enqueued in `functions.php` only for `is_shop() || is_product_taxonomy() || is_search()` (defer, depends on jquery); skipped if the Quick Order shim (`dpQoFilterParamPatched`) or this one already patched it; does nothing if the WBW object or method is missing. Remove it when a WBW update fixes `getFilterParam`.
- **Local QA (localhost, Chrome, no orders):** shop, `product-category/lifestyle/` and `/brand/24bottles/`: card, headings, checkboxes, chips verified at 1440 and 390 px (off-canvas mobile panel shown), no horizontal overflow; selecting a filter, removing it through the chip x (category and attribute chips), Clear All, keyboard Tab/Space on the checkbox with the focus ring: all work with 0 console errors. Quick Order page: the theme script and `.products-lhs-filter` are absent, the default WBW checkbox is unchanged.
- **Remaining differences from Figma (deferred):** no "FILTERI" caption in the card (needs a template change); active chips stay inside the card instead of the "Aktivni filteri" row above the content (template change); WBW prints "+ Show More" in English instead of "Prikazi vise (8)"; WBW English strings "In Stock" / "Out of Stock" (WBW settings, out of scope); chip x is still a non-focusable div (WBW markup); `.wfpDescription` is hidden for every WBW block (a real description would not show).
- **Not verified:** staging render and behaviour (pending), Safari / iOS, screen reader. Observation, not caused by this work: on the localhost Quick Order page the QO shim flag `dpQoFilterParamPatched` was not set (QO init returns early when `window.dpQuickOrder` lacks `cartSyncUrl` / `wpNonce` / `productsUrl`); not investigated.
- **Status:** B2 / T9 IMPLEMENTED - AWAITING STAGING / OWNER VERIFICATION. C1: only the filter-sidebar portion is covered; C1 is not closed (sort bar, pagination and other archive parts were not audited).

Verification = owner confirmation only; not claimed: Safari / iOS, screen-reader announcement, real keyboard activation on the guest login and `approval-pending`.

The other G4 residuals stay open: search placeholder 2.75:1 and search border 1.92:1 contrast, touch targets < 44 px, no screen-reader test (`figma-alignment.md` sections 29-30).

## 2026-11-10 (G4 skip-link target) - IMPLEMENTED AND LOCALLY CHECKED; COMMITTED FOR STAGING DEPLOY; AWAITING STAGING / OWNER VERIFICATION (NOT CLOSED)

Accessibility residual from `figma-alignment.md` section 30 ("the skip link target `#primary-content` is not focusable"): the skip link now really moves keyboard focus to the main-content target, and every page has exactly one `id="primary-content"`.
- **Root cause (two parts):** the targets were plain `div` / `main` elements without `tabindex="-1"`, so focus stayed at the page start; and `header.php` printed `<div class="inner-page" id="primary-content">` while `index.php`, `page.php` and `page-segment-landing.php` always printed their own `<main id="primary-content">`, giving two elements with the same id on the home page, cart, checkout, My Account and Quick Order pages (pre-existing).
- **Change:** `tabindex="-1"` on the skip-link targets (`header.php` Quick Order and standard wrappers, the three `<main>` templates, `approval-pending.php`); new stateless helper `dreampoint_b2b_header_renders_main_target()` (`inc/template-tags.php`) is used by `header.php` (replaces the inline condition) and by the three page templates, which now print the id/tabindex on `<main>` only when the header does not render the wrapper (standalone auth screens, guest login); scoped rule `#primary-content[tabindex="-1"]:focus { outline: 0 }` in `sass/components/_header.scss` (without it Chrome showed a focus ring after activation); generated `style.css` rebuilt (single added rule). Semantic `<main>` elements, layout and skip-link appearance unchanged; `$is_standalone_auth` in `header.php` is now unused and intentionally left in place.
- **Local verification (Chrome):** exactly one `#primary-content` on home, shop, brand, contact, cart, checkout, My Account, Quick Order, lifestyle and lost-password; real Enter on the skip link moves focus to the target on home, shop and lost-password; click activation on cart, checkout, My Account, Quick Order and shop; no outline, no overflow, console 0 errors, no PHP warnings. Guest login / lost-password markup checked via HTTP (one `<main id="primary-content" tabindex="-1">`).
- **Not verified:** real keyboard activation on the guest login and `approval-pending` (needs a logged-out or pending-user state), Safari / iOS focus behaviour, screen-reader announcement, staging render (pending at the time of this entry).

## 2026-11-06 (PDP visual follow-ups: Select2, sticky gallery, Back-in-Stock form) - COMPLETE / STAGING VERIFIED / OWNER ACCEPTED

Commit `a3e48556ad184ec42dfcc7eaf8a9ed6ab41a35f3` ("style(pdp): align product controls and back-in-stock form"; parent `c61a48d`), pushed to `origin/master` and deployed to staging by `git merge --ff-only` as the site user `dream9399` (staging HEAD = `a3e4855`, tree clean, new files owned by `dream9399`). Five files under `themes/dreampoint-b2b/`: `back-in-stock-notifier-for-woocommerce/default-form.php` (new), `sass/pages/shop-single.scss`, generated `css/pages/shop-single.css`, `js/product-single.js`, `woocommerce/content-single-product.php`. Staging browser QA: PASS. The project owner personally verified the staging result and accepted all three items on 2026-11-06. The project has no production environment (theme `CLAUDE.md`); nothing was deployed beyond staging.
- **A. Select2 option density:** PDP-scoped rule `body.single-product .select2-dropdown .select2-results__option` (the panel is appended to `<body>`; the variation selects are the only Select2 instances on the PDP): Montserrat 14/400, line-height 18, padding 8/12 -> 34 px single-line options (was 18/300, 48 px); long labels wrap; colours and states unchanged.
- **B. Sticky gallery + description:** the Description tabs section is now rendered inside `.product-main-info > .row`, and gallery + info columns are wrapped in `.product-main-cols` (`content-single-product.php`). From 1200 px the row is a grid and the wrapper is `display: contents`, so the gallery spans both grid rows and stays sticky through the description (it releases at the end of the row, before the USP strip; tabs stay 485 px under the info column). Below 1200 px the wrapper is the former wrapping flex row, the tabs wrap full width under it and the gallery is confined to the wrapper (a plain grid was rejected: Chrome ignores the grid area for sticky and the gallery floated over the description). Below 768 px sticky stays off.
- **C. Back-in-Stock form (Figma PDP-02 node `11148:32975`, plugin Back in Stock Notifier for WooCommerce 7.5.2):** supported theme override `back-in-stock-notifier-for-woocommerce/default-form.php` (plugin `CWG_Template::locate_template`); no name field, no Bootstrap panel, heading and email label kept as screen-reader-only text; one row email input (297 x 48, `#fcfcfd`, 1 px `#d2d6db`, radius 6, Montserrat 16/400, placeholder "email") + Primary XL button "Obavijesti me" (176 x 48); the button wraps below the input (full width) when the row is narrower than ~384 px (input min basis 200 + gap 8 + button 176; measured: one line at 481/449 px, stacked at 329/343 px). All plugin mechanics are kept (`.cwginstock-subscribe-form`, `.cwgstock_email`, hidden product/variation ids, nonce, `.cwgstock_button`, `.cwgstock_output`, all `cwg_instock_*` hooks); the optional phone input is rendered only when the plugin's "Show Phone" setting is on (the plugin JS needs `.cwgstock_phone` / `cwginstock_iti` in that case). `syncBackInStockRow()` in `js/product-single.js` toggles `.dp-bis-active` on `.single_variation_wrap` from the existing `show_variation` / `hide_variation` events only when the displayed form's `cwg-variation-id` equals the current `variation_id`; CSS hides the quantity + Add-to-cart row then. The row is only hidden, never removed. R1 guard preserved. Plugin core untouched.
- **Verified:** pre-commit (local): native Enter in the email field clicks the Back-in-Stock button (first submit button in the form), never Add-to-cart; with a verified network interceptor the plugin's AJAX attempt was only recorded and blocked. Staging (`office-reading` frozen-blue/dioptrija-3, `secrid-novcanik-miniwallet-matte` desert, `c-reading`, `c-sun`): form only for the selected unavailable variation, available/unavailable transitions, reset, rapid reset/clearing, sticky gallery at 1440/1024/768/390, Select2 14/400 34 px, console 0 errors.
- **Limitations (documented, not newly discovered failures; they do not invalidate the owner's acceptance):** accepted **R1-T1** (rapid switching between different valid variations can briefly show stale content or mismatched controls; the Back-in-Stock row logic is not affected by a stale form of another variation). NOT fully verified: the real Back-in-Stock submission (AJAX, server processing, notification e-mail); the optional phone field with the setting enabled in a real browser (source/stub-render evidence only); a simple out-of-stock product (no suitable fixture locally or on staging). Configuration note: on staging the plugin option `form_title` still holds "Email when stock available", so the screen-reader-only heading is English there; plugin settings/translations were not changed. Staging plugin settings (read-only): phone, name-hide, force-plugin-template, quantity, captcha and GDPR options are all unset.
