## 2026-11-06 (C2-4 F1/F2/F3 + R1 follow-up) - COMMITTED `c61a48d` / ON STAGING / STAGING QA PASSED / R1-T1 OWNER-ACCEPTED LIMITATION

Phase A + B of C2-4 are on staging as `e1b0b93` (owner review pending, per the C2-4 checkpoint). This follow-up was first verified locally, then committed and pushed as `c61a48d` ("fix(pdp): stabilize variation controls and stale reset state", six files incl. this file) and deployed to staging by fast-forward; staging browser QA passed (variable/unavailable/max=1/reset/rapid reset, simple products, 1440/768/390). **Status update 2026-11-06 (later):** the original text below was written while the work was still local; the "LOCAL ONLY / UNCOMMITTED" wording and the "Next step" line are superseded by this paragraph. An explicit owner acceptance of C2-4 itself is NOT recorded in this entry (SUPERSEDED: recorded 2026-11-06 in the C2-4 closure entry above; C2-4 is CLOSED). Parent C2 stays PARTIAL; C2-4 stays OPEN (see the PDP visual follow-ups entry above for the later, separately accepted work).
- **Files (all under `themes/dreampoint-b2b/`):** `sass/pages/shop-single.scss`, generated `css/pages/shop-single.css`, `js/product-single.js`, `template-parts/pdp-quantity.php`, `woocommerce/single-product/add-to-cart/variation-add-to-cart-button.php`.
- **F1/F2/F3:** disabled Add-to-cart keeps the enabled tokens with `opacity: 0.6` (WooCommerce `.disabled` class kept, alerts preserved); quantity follows the Add-to-cart state (`readOnly` + `aria-disabled`, plus/minus natively disabled, `.number--locked` opacity 0.6); variable products render disabled/locked on first paint. Owner approved the local visual result.
- **R1 defect (confirmed, WooCommerce 11.2.2):** `onFoundVariation` (`add-to-cart-variation.js` ~441) schedules a 300 ms `show_variation` with no cancellation. A reset or an incomplete selection inside that window is followed by a stale `onShow` that re-enabled Add-to-cart, unlocked quantity and re-showed the old variation block, with `variation_id` empty. Reproduced locally on `dev-toneri-i-tinte-var-020` (M/Black then "Očisti" or clearing one attribute within ~50 ms).
- **R1 mitigation (local, `js/product-single.js` only):** `isQuantityLocked()` also locks while `variation_id` is empty/0; a delegated `show_variation` handler re-emits WooCommerce's own `hide_variation` on `.single_variation` when `variation_id` is empty, so `onHide` restores the disabled/selection-needed state. No timers, polling, MutationObserver or core override. Verified locally: rapid reset, rapid attribute clearing, delayed reset, reset + valid reselect (final state correct).
- **R1-T1 — ACCEPTED LIMITATION (owner decision, 2026-11-06):** while the user switches quickly between DIFFERENT variations, an older `show_variation` can fire after the current `found_variation`, so for roughly 100-130 ms (the gap between the user's two selections; not guaranteed to be bounded) the variation block may show the previous variation's price/availability, and the Add-to-cart state may be wrong: valid -> unavailable can show it enabled, unavailable -> valid can show it disabled. Runtime evidence (local, event log): the last `show_variation` always matched the current `variation_id`; final state correct in every tested scenario. NOT fixed on purpose: an ID-mismatch guard cannot reuse `hide_variation` (its theme handler resets the Description tab and only `found_variation` restores it) and restoring the button would need a copy of WooCommerce's purchasability logic. No further mitigation approved.
- **Server-side safeguards (source only):** the form submits the current `variation_id`; `WC_Form_Handler::add_to_cart_handler_variable` rejects an empty id (`class-wc-form-handler.php:1177`) and `WC_Cart::add_to_cart` checks purchasable, in stock and enough stock (`class-wc-cart.php` ~1368-1397). Add-to-cart was NEVER submitted during QA, including in the transient state.
- **Not verified:** the AJAX variation path (> 30 variations) was source-reviewed only (fixture uses local variation data; xhr is aborted on a new request); keyboard-driven R1 timing; the "D2 sequence" named in earlier QA notes has no recoverable definition in the docs, so it is NOT VERIFIED (the max=1 -> reset behaviour was checked as an approximation only).
- **Final local QA (2026-11-06, no submit/cart/DB):** variable `dev-toneri-i-tinte-var-020`: initial, M/Black (max 56), XS/Black (max 1), S/White unavailable (WooCommerce alert text shown), normal/rapid reset, rapid attribute clearing, reset + reselect L/White, quantity lock/unlock, opacity 0.6 vs 1 - PASS. Simple `dev-alati-i-oprema-0221` (max 10) and `dev-led-rasvjeta-0316` (max 1) unaffected - PASS. 1440/768/390: no overflow, button 313/329/343 x 48, quantity 160 px, rapid-reset and valid/reset states correct - PASS. `node --check`, PHP lint of both templates, `git diff --check` - PASS; console 0 errors.
- **Next step (superseded):** this was the original post-QA plan (commit/push, staging deploy, staging QA, owner review). Commit/push/deploy/staging QA are done (see the paragraph under the heading); owner review of C2-4 itself and closing the C2-4 docs remain open.

## 2026-11-05 (C2-4 audit) - AUDIT COMPLETE — IMPLEMENTATION AWAITING APPROVAL

Read-only audit of the PDP quantity control, add-to-cart button and variation selectors against Figma PDP-01/02 (nodes `11148:32808` group, `11276:47876` quantity, `11276:25632` button, `11148:32954` variations). Nothing implemented; staging was only read. Parent C2 stays PARTIAL.
- **Figma (measured):** quantity 160x48, border ~1.4 px `#d2d6db`, radius 16; cells: left arrow 48x48 (disabled: border-right ~1 px `#e5e7eb`), value cell 61 px (Montserrat Regular 22 px as drawn, ~24 px effective after the 44->48 instance scale, `#6c727e`), right arrow 48x48 (border-left ~1 px `#d2d6db`), icons 24 px. Button `flex:1` (313 of 481 px), h48, radius 24, `#0d121c`, padding 14/24, Montserrat 600 18 px, cart icon 20 px, 8 px gap to the quantity. Variations: label Montserrat 600 12 px (-0.24 px, `#0d121c`) + 8 px + pill row (gap 16; pill 8x6 padding, radius 4, 12 px/600; unselected `#f3f4f6`, selected `#0d121c` with `#f9fafb` text), block top padding 24. No mobile frames.
- **Staging (1440 px):** quantity = `span.minus` + WooCommerce quantity input + `span.plus` (`woocommerce/single-product/add-to-cart/simple.php`, `variation-add-to-cart-button.php`, `js/product-single.js`): 104x48, 1 px border, radius 0, cells 32x46 without dividers, icons 16 px, value 16 px `#0d121c`; button 369x48 (width follows the quantity), icon icomoon `\e918` (shopping bag; Figma: cart). Variations: native `table.variations` with select2 (label 18 px weight 300, select 46 px; single-option attributes preselected and hidden via `dp-single-option`). 768 px: button 217 px wide and 60 px tall (label wraps); 390 px: +/- cells 32x46 (< 44 px tap target); no horizontal overflow at 1440/1024/768/390; 1024 px rows are tight.
- **Existing functional issues:** (1) `updateQuantityButtons` searches `.minus`/`.plus` inside the nearest `.quantity`, but they are siblings in `.number`, so `.disabled` is never set (verified at 1, 2 and max 41; min/max clamping and the max toast do work). (2) +/- are `span`s without `tabindex`/`role`/`aria-label` (not keyboard operable; the input has `aria-label`). (3) `max=1` products (e.g. `carape-lava-lamp`) get WooCommerce's hidden quantity input, so only the arrows show.
- **Proposed scope:** Phase A (quantity + button, ~5 h): SCSS (width, radius, border, dividers, icon/value size and colour, button width, tap targets), fix the selector in `product-single.js`, `<button>` with `aria-label` in the two templates. Phase B (pill variations, ~8 h): pills rendered over the native select (select2 stays the source of truth), kept in sync with `change`, `reset_variations`, `found_variation`, WooCommerce-disabled options, the AJAX lookup (> 30 variations) and preselected single options. Total ~13 h. Risks: pill/select2 sync, quantity width affecting the 768 px button, colours with up to 11 options.
- **Open owner decisions:** (1) which attributes get pills (Dioptrija/small sets only vs all; Boja has up to 11 options); (2) cart icon asset instead of the bag; (3) quantity number 22 vs ~24 px; (4) static "1" for `max=1` products; (5) disabled style of the add-to-cart button before a variation is selected (undefined in Figma). Limitation: element screenshots fail in the hidden automation tab, so everything was measured from the DOM.

### 2026-11-05 (variation descriptions in the Description tab + stable USP gap) - IMPLEMENTED — TECHNICALLY VERIFIED (owner visually confirmed, see C2-3 closure above)

Code `11be92d` (staging only, `git pull --ff-only` from `9b5d650`; served CSS/JS MD5 equal to local). Files: `inc/woocommerce.php`, `woocommerce/content-single-product.php`, `js/product-single.js`, `sass/pages/shop-single.scss` + generated `css/pages/shop-single.css`.
- **Rules (owner-approved):** parent description = default and fallback; the selected variation's description overrides it; no description anywhere = Description tab hidden; reset restores the initial state; never stale or duplicated content.
- **PHP:** `dreampoint_b2b_product_tabs()` (still Description-only, ADR-028) now also registers the native Description tab (same `woocommerce_product_description_tab` callback/template) for a variable product with an empty parent description when `dreampoint_b2b_has_variation_description()` finds a description on a visible variation (`get_available_variations('objects')`, stops at the first hit). Filter `dreampoint_b2b_variation_description_payload()` (`woocommerce_available_variation`, priority 20) copies WooCommerce's formatted `variation_description` to `dp_variation_description` and empties the native property, so the native `.woocommerce-variation-description` renders an empty (0 px) node. Targeted check: the only consumer of `variation_description` is WooCommerce's own `variation.php` template (theme and active plugins searched).

- **JS (`product-single.js`):** `setDescription()` captures the server-rendered parent HTML once (everything except the `<h2>Opis</h2>`), rebuilds the panel as `<h2>` + HTML only when the content changes (idempotent; WooCommerce fires the variation events several times) and toggles the section's `hidden`. Called on `found_variation` (`dp_variation_description`), `reset_data` and `hide_variation` (parent state). The same server-formatted HTML WooCommerce itself inserts is used; no manual line breaks.
- **Spacing (replaces the `:has()` rule of `9b5d650`):** `#product-single .features { margin-top: 40px }` at >= 768 px for every state, `.wc-tabs-section { padding-bottom: 0 }` at >= 768 px (it had 50-64 px from `.block`), `.wc-tabs-section[hidden] { display: none }`. Widths 1200/1024/768 (`office-reading`, hidden and with variation): gap 40, text -> strip 40, Features padding 24/24, no overflow; 390: gap 0 / 32 px (unchanged), no overflow. `pocetna`: 64/64, margin 0. Console 0 errors.

### 2026-11-05 (C2-3 spacing check) - NO CODE CHANGE (owner decision)

Owner hypothesis: Figma needs ~40 px more space between `#product-single-main` and `.features`. Measured at 1440 px (Figma PDP-01 `11148:32726` and PDP-02 `11148:32859`, identical; staging `carape-lava-lamp`, `437ed4d`): Figma description text bottom y=1036, USP frame `11148:32836`/`11148:33003` top y=1076 (gap 40), first icon y=1100 (24 px padding), i.e. text -> icons 64 px, USP frame bottom 1256 = related section top. Staging: text bottom 1017, `.features` top 1081 (gap 64, from the 64 px bottom padding of `.wc-tabs-section`, which sits INSIDE `#product-single-main`, so the element-to-element gap `#product-single-main` -> `.features` is 0), first icon 1105, i.e. text -> icons 88 px; `.features` bottom = related top (matches Figma). So staging is 24 px looser than Figma, not 40 px tighter; the "40 px" is the Figma frame gap measured against a different boundary. The owner chose "do not change anything" (alternative offered: `.wc-tabs-section` padding-bottom 64 -> 40 px at >= 1200 px, PDP only, would give Figma's 64 px). Part of the open "tabs vertical gap" item; C2-3 stays OWNER VISUAL QA PENDING.

A separate block field group `group_6a704ca216bac` ("Block / Company Features") belongs to the block. `group_67518f7e6ea48` has NO database post on staging (JSON-only, 0 acf-field posts), so a future field removal is a JSON edit; do not delete stored option values.

## 2026-11-05 (C2-2 closure + PDP EAN) - C2-2 CLOSED / TECHNICALLY VERIFIED / OWNER VISUALLY CONFIRMED; PDP EAN IMPLEMENTED / TECHNICALLY VERIFIED / OWNER VISUAL QA PENDING

**C2-2 closed.** The owner manually confirmed the final staging result (`b4b14c1`): gallery arrows appearance, gallery border/radius, sticky behaviour, and arrow usability on a real mobile device. Implementation: `ab58fdc` (vertical 4-thumbnail rail, 4:3 image, fluid 1200-1399 px) and `b4b14c1` (border/radius, subtle hover arrows, header-aware sticky offset); technical QA (Slick sync, wrap-around, variation image swap, Fancybox, breakpoint transitions) passed. The earlier automated-screenshot and touch-emulation limitations stay historical QA notes, not open blockers. No automated pixel-by-pixel Figma comparison was done. Parent C2 stays PARTIAL; C2-3 (USP strip) and C2-4 (quantity stepper, variation pills) stay OPEN, as do the other PDP differences (share row, OOS price, "Povezani proizvodi" heading, ~80 px top offset, tabs vertical gap).

**PDP EAN (code `a44293a`; `woocommerce/content-single-product.php`, `inc/product-search.php`, `js/product-single.js`, `sass/pages/shop-single.scss`, generated `css/pages/shop-single.css`).** Staging only, `git pull --ff-only` (b4b14c1 -> a44293a, tree clean, MD5 of css/js/php equal to local). Figma PDP-01 `11148:32794`: one flex row, "Kataloški broj: ..." and "EAN: ..." side by side, 32 px gap, 8 px above / 64 px below the row, Montserrat 500 14/20 -0.154 px, `text-secondary`; the EAN is beside the catalog number, not below it.
- **Data source (unchanged ERP/importer):** Apros `barcode` -> importer `set_global_unique_id()` -> WooCommerce native `_global_unique_id`. Simple products: `$product->get_global_unique_id()` rendered server-side. Variable parents carry no EAN (no parent value is ever shown); the selected variation's EAN comes from the new `woocommerce_available_variation` filter `dreampoint_b2b_variation_ean()` (`dp_global_unique_id`, next to the existing `dp_catalog_number` filter).
- **Markup/JS:** `.product-sku` is now a `div` with two `.product-sku__item` children (`--catalog` keeps `.product-sku__value`, `--ean` has `.product-ean__value`; `data-default` holds the server value). `setSkuItem()` in `product-single.js` fills the value, hides an empty item (a label never shows without a value) and hides the whole row when both are empty; `found_variation` sets catalog number (existing logic) and EAN (empty = hidden, no parent fallback); `reset_data` restores both defaults (EAN hides again on a variable parent). `h1:has(+ .product-sku:not([hidden]))` keeps the 0/20 px title margin correct when the row is hidden. 64 px (desktop) / 20 px (<768 px) belongs to the whole row, not per item.
- **Staging QA:** `carape-lava-lamp` (simple): "Kataloški broj: 102040" + "EAN: 8436602102040" on one row at 1440 (items 150 + 32 gap + 147 px, row 8/64 px, font 14/20/500/-0.154 px, #384250). `cruise-screen-black-0` (out of stock): row "Kataloški broj: SCR/CRUISE/B/+0 EAN: 3701210444031", OOS text, no price, no add-to-cart, unchanged. `c-sun` (variable): all 10 variations expose `dp_global_unique_id`; initial state shows catalog number only (EAN hidden); real variation selections (3 of 10) show the matching EAN and catalog number; reset restores catalog number and hides EAN. Synthetic `found_variation`: variation without EAN hides the EAN item and keeps the row; both values empty hides the row and the title margin returns to 20 px. Responsive on `c-sun` (variation with a 10-char catalog number + EAN): 1440/1200/1024 same line (1200 and 1024: row 64 px below), 768 and 390 wrap naturally to two lines (390: 20 px below), no overflow at any width; gallery at 1200 unchanged (571 / 427x320, 4 thumbs). Console 0 errors. Screenshot (cruise-screen-black-0, 1440) shows the row matching the Figma structure; no pixel-level comparison.
- **Limitations / pending:** owner visual confirmation of the EAN row. Variations without an EAN were verified with synthetic events only (staging: 1290/1291 variations have an EAN; ID 14558 of parent 14556 has none). EAN is not added to product cards, search, checkout, e-mails or structured data (out of scope).

Header facts (measured): `#header` is `position:sticky`, 72 px tall at every width >= 768, its own `top` = admin-bar height via `body.admin-bar #header` (32 px; 46 px <= 782 px). New `top = 72 + 24 px gap` (96), `body.admin-bar` 32 + 72 + 24 (128), <= 782 px 46 + 72 + 24 (142); constants `$dp-pdp-header-height` / `$dp-pdp-sticky-gap` in shop-single.scss (the header height is a literal in `_header.scss`, not a token). Short viewports: `position:static` when height <= 600 px (>= 1200 px) or <= 780 px (768-1199 px). Pure CSS; no JS.

Overlap only shows on products with a tall info column (many options, long title). Tested by forcing the info column to 1500/1800 px in the browser.

## 2026-11-04 (brands marquee, wide viewports) - Right-edge coverage gap FIXED ON STAGING

Code `dc2c20d` (`js/slick-init.js`, brands block only; not bundled, no build). Staging only. Resolves the "symptom B" recorded in the entry below.

- **Root cause (verified in `slick.min.js` + geometry):** `setupInfinite` appends clones only for indexes `< slideCount` (the extra `slidesToShow` iterations clone non-existent slides), so after the loop reset the track holds exactly ONE set of logos past the current slide. 9 logos = 1601 px, shorter than the list at 1920 px (1905) and 2560 px (2545) -> empty right edge (measured at 1920: 163 px at `currentSlide 8`, 376 px raw at `currentSlide 0`; static model: 341 px at 1920, 981 px at 2560, 0 at <= 1440). Images were loaded; not a loading problem.
- **Options:** (A) Slick config (`slidesToShow` N): rejected, it only adds PREPENDED clones, the appended count stays `slideCount`. (B) selected: before init, repeat the existing logo sequence inside the brands slider until one set covers `max(screen.width, innerWidth)` (max 5 copies). Same links/order/images, no template, SCSS, image or vendor change.
- **Details:** pre-init lazy images have width 0, so each logo width is derived from `width`/`height` attributes and the computed CSS `max-width`/`max-height` (text brands measured directly; estimated set 1565 px vs actual 1601 px, errs on the safe side). Copies get `aria-hidden="true"`, `data-dp-repeat`, links `tabindex=-1` (re-applied after Slick's `initADA` on `afterChange`/`reInit`, same pattern as segments); no ids. DOM: 1440/768/390 unchanged (9 + 10 clones); 1920 and 2560 = 18 + 19 clones.
- **Measured on the deployed file (fresh visible tab, 2375/2375 samples, a full cycle of all 18 positions incl. the loop reset):** 2560 and 1920: max uncovered width 0 px (raw gap never above the normal 72 px logo spacing); speed 59.0-60.4 px/s over 32 transitions; one reset jump (`x -3448 -> -248`) to an identical position. 1440 / 768 / 390: static uncovered 0, speed 59.2-60.7 px/s over 7 transitions each, no horizontal overflow. Reduced motion (1920, emulated): paused and `swipe` on at load, resumes on no-preference, pauses again after the in-flight transition; all logos loaded. Pre-init vs post-init brands height 51 px / segments unchanged; segments + brands initialise; no console errors.
- **NOT VERIFIED:** human visual observation (measurement only); 1920 at DPR 2 (not supported by the tool); browser zoom-out or resizing beyond the initial `max(screen.width, innerWidth)` after load (copies are computed once at init; a reload re-evaluates); keyboard tab order in a real screen reader.

## 2026-11-04 (brands marquee) - Constant scrolling speed + reduced motion, DEPLOYED ON STAGING (symptom B open)

Code `dd5df50` (`js/slick-init.js`, brands block only; the file is enqueued directly, not bundled, no build). Staging only.

- **Cause of variable speed:** `autoplaySpeed: 0` makes Slick chain one-slide transitions back to back, each with the fixed `speed: 3000`, so px/s = (slide width + 72 px margins) / 3 s (about 42-71 px/s before).
- **Fix:** a `beforeChange` handler sets `slick.options.speed = |getLeft(cur+slidesToScroll) - getLeft(cur)| / 60 * 1000` ms. Verified in `slick.min.js`: `slideHandler` triggers `beforeChange` BEFORE `animateSlide` -> `applyTransition` (and the `setTimeout`) read `options.speed`, so the value applies to the same transition. No vendor/global patching, no timers.
- **Reduced motion:** `matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)')`: `slickPause` + swipe/touchMove enabled; switching back calls `slickPlay` and disables swipe again; reacts to live preference changes. JS only.
- **Measured on the deployed file (visible tab, 450-900 of 450-900 samples visible):** 1920, 1440, 768, 390 all 59.2-60.3 px/s for slide widths 53/57/77/105/140 px (durations 2.083-3.533 s); the infinite-loop reset (clone -> slide 0, `x -1848 -> -249`) is a single jump to an identical position; transition sample counts match durations (no pause above about 80 ms measurable); no horizontal overflow; segments + brands initialise; no console errors. Reduced motion (1440, emulated): paused at load (x constant 6 s), swipe on, logos loaded; no-preference resumes; reduce again pauses after the in-flight transition finishes.
- **Symptom B reproduced by measurement (not visually) at 1920 x 1080, DPR 1:** the track runs out of slides at the right edge: gap up to 163 px at `currentSlide = 8` (last child = clone slick-index 17, the end of the track) and up to 376 px at `currentSlide = 0`; at 1440 the gap never exceeds the normal 72 px. All images were loaded (`complete`, `naturalWidth > 0`), so it is NOT a loading/decoding problem: 9 real logos total only 1601 px and Slick adds 1 + 9 clones (19 slides), too few for a 1905 px list. This predates the speed change (same slide set). Fixed afterwards by `dc2c20d` (see the entry above); was left open here (e.g. enough clones/duplicate set for wide viewports; the template currently pads to 8 items only).
- **NOT VERIFIED:** human visual observation (measurement only); DPR 2/3 at 1920; swipe gesture itself was not exercised.

## 2026-11-04 (homepage sliders) - Segments/Brands pre-Slick layout, DEPLOYED ON STAGING - PARTIALLY COMPLETE, NOT FULLY VERIFIED

Code `12137ba` (CSS only: `sass/blocks/segments-section.scss`, `sass/blocks/brands.scss`, generated `css/blocks/segments-section.css`, `css/blocks/brands.css`). Staging only.

- **Root cause (measured, staging, authenticated):** `.segments-slider` and `.brands-slider` had no pre-init layout. `.slick-slider { opacity:0 }` does not apply before Slick adds the class, and Slick initialises only after the deferred scripts (~1.1 s on a cold load). Before init the segment boxes stacked at full container width (1440: tile 1200x1791, slider 1200x5372, document 7256 px; 768: tile 690x1030; 390: slider 1535) and the brand items stacked vertically (342 px, then 51 px).
- **Fix:** `:not(.slick-initialized)` pre-init rules, the same pattern as `hero.scss`. Segments: flex row `width: calc(100% + 24px)`, `margin: 0 -12px`, boxes `flex: 0 0 calc(100%/3)` with 12 px side padding (mirrors `.slick-list`/`.slick-slide`), 50% at `max-width: 990px` and 100% at `766px` (Slick applies its responsive settings for window width < 991 / < 767), `padding-bottom: 50px` at <= 990 for the dots. Brands: single `nowrap` flex row, `overflow: hidden`, `min-height: 51px`, items `flex: 0 0 auto`, margin `0 36px` (first item 0 left). No JS, template or Slick-default changes.
- **Geometry after deploy (pre-init vs post-init, deployed CSS, hashes of both CSS files equal to the commit):** segments tile identical at 1440 (384x573 @x113), 768 (333x497 @x32), 390 (343x512 @x16); brands slider 51 px before and after at all three; first brand logo x = 0 before and after; no horizontal overflow; document height equal at 768 (2922) and 390 (3152); at 1440 the first sample was 2465 vs 2486 post-init (21 px, sampled before the brands row had a laid-out first item; not attributed to the sliders).
- **NOT VERIFIED:** visual QA. The controlled browser tab reported `visibilityState: hidden` in the filmstrip run, so screenshots do not prove what a user sees; no hard-reload/throttled run was possible (CDP emulation blocked). Brands continuous autoplay could not be confirmed (Slick pauses autoplay in a hidden tab: track transform unchanged); segments arrows/dots navigation not exercised (3 slides at 1440; 768/390 dots not clicked). No console errors during the measured loads. Owner should visually confirm a normal and a hard reload at 1440/768/390.

## 2026-11-03 (B1.1) - Product card images: uncropped `card-loop`, COMPLETE - VERIFIED ON STAGING (closed 2026-11-04)

## 2026-11-03 (B1) - Product card Figma alignment, VISUAL/UI VERIFIED ON STAGING

Code `ecfa98c` (`woocommerce/content-product.php`, `sass/components/_content.scss`, generated `style.css`) and `8102596` (slider wrapper). Figma Product card `11148:32832`. Staging only.

- **Image strategy (owner decision):** `.photo-holder { aspect-ratio: 282/248 }` with `img { width/height: 100%; object-fit: contain }`, border removed, `max-width: 340px` removed. Reason: the `product-loop` source is a stored 340x462 hard crop; a pixel analysis of 120 products showed that `cover` at the Figma ratio would cut 16-31% of the product height for 50 of them (bottles, lanyards, notebooks), while `contain` keeps every product whole (wide products appear smaller). Suplementi (reference only) uses the same wrapper + `contain` pattern with a soft-resized `product-loop`. Switching to `cover` is a one-word change in `_content.scss`; thumbnails/media settings were not touched.
- **Layout:** container `16px 8px` padding, 8px gap, title slot `min-height: 42px`, `.price-action` flex row (price left via `order`, 32px action right, `min-height: 48px`; legacy 30px `.action-holder` padding removed, pointer-events behaviour kept), `del` above the price. DOM order unchanged.
- **Badge:** `<span class="stock-badge">Na stanju</span>` when `!$out_of_stock` (the card's existing `is_in_stock()`; for variable products WooCommerce's synced parent status = at least one variation available). No `get_available_variations()`, no APROS call, no quantity (ADR-027). Colours from `$dp-color-success` / `$dp-color-success-strong`. Out-of-stock cards keep "Nema na stanju" and no badge.
- **Regression found and fixed in QA (`8102596`):** the PDP "Predlozeno za vas" Slick slider rendered three cards at 132px and one at 285px, because Slick's content wrapper (`.slider-grid .slick-slide > div`, a non-stretched flex item) shrink-wrapped to the intrinsic width of the not-yet-loaded lazy images. Fix: `width: 100%` on that wrapper (one rule; also protects the homepage block templates, which share `.slider-grid`).
- **Verified on staging (real browser, computed styles):** shop at 1440/1024/768/390 (12 cards: 12 variable, 2 out of stock, 10 badges; photo 277x244 / 218x191 / 217x191 / 164x144; card 277x399 at 1440 vs Figma 282x402; button 32x32 at the right edge, no collision, no overflow, uniform row heights); category `boce-i-posude-za-hranu`, brand `24bottles` and search `naocale` at 1440/390 (search includes simple in-stock and simple out-of-stock cards: AJAX add-to-cart markup intact, badge only on the in-stock one); PDP related slider at all four widths after the fix (equal slides, 4/4 badges, no overflow); hit-tests: action button receives the click, area beside it falls through to the card link; no console errors. No click on an add-to-cart control, no data change.
- **NOT VERIFIED:** sale cards (`del`/`ins`, no sale prices exist), placeholder image, homepage product sections (0 cards rendered, but the shared slider fix covers them), cart cross-sells (cart has none), keyboard focus ring on the action button in this pass (rules unchanged), 768/1024 on category/brand/search (measured 1440/390 there). At 390 the out-of-stock label wraps to two lines next to the button (row 52px; Figma has no out-of-stock state).

## 2026-11-03 (form focus refinement) - Soft Gray Focus for form fields, VERIFIED ON STAGING

Code `082ae36` (`sass/theme/_vars.scss`, `sass/theme/_mixins.scss`, `sass/components/_content.scss`, `sass/pages/checkout.scss` + generated `style.css`, `css/pages/checkout.css`). Owner decision (Variant B): form fields get a soft gray focus; buttons and links keep the strong monochrome ring; errors stay red. This supersedes the form-field part of the "global form focus" section below (`e87931e`/`a49c384`); the earlier shared architecture is kept.

- **Two explicit mixins:** `dp-focus-ring` (white 2px + black 4px) stays for buttons / links (`button-figma-base` and the header/footer rules, unchanged output); new `dp-form-focus-ring` = `border-color: $dp-color-focus-field` (`#475467`, new token in `_vars.scss`) + `box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(#475467, .16)` for FORM CONTROLS only. It sets `border-color` itself, so it is included on the element that owns the border (the Select2 container, not the inner `.select2-selection`).
- **Migrated to `dp-form-focus-ring`:** legacy `.custom-form` inputs, `div.custom-form:not(.subscription-form)` inputs / selects / textareas (login, register, lost password, My Account, addresses, CF7), Select2 container focus/open, the Select2 dropdown search field, and the Blocks checkout text inputs / country-state selects. The CSS diff of `style.css` contained only those four rules (plus the checkout one); no button/link rule changed.
- **State priority unchanged:** red invalid rules (including Select2 and Blocks `has-error` / `aria-invalid`) still outrank the gray focus; `:disabled` untouched; the Contact Select2 option states (white / `#f3f4f6` / `#171717` selected) were not touched.
- **Verified (real browser, computed styles, settled transitions):** every focused field reads border `rgb(71, 84, 103)` + `rgba(71, 84, 103, 0.16) 0 0 0 3px`, outline none: Contact textarea and Select2 (1440/768/390, Select2 open + option states intact, invalid + focused stays red), Blocks checkout email / name / street / country / state (1440, 390; error variants red when `has-error` is added non-persistently), My Account billing address (10 fields incl. Select2 country/state), guest login, lost-password and registration (1440 and 390, isolated context). The Contact submit button still shows the strong ring on keyboard focus (`255,255,255 2px + 13,18,28 4px`). No stylesheet rule with `#c7d7fe` on Contact / Checkout; no horizontal overflow; no console errors.
- **Visibility note (not changed, spec kept):** the 16% ring alone is faint on white and almost invisible on the `#f3f4f6` login/register background (about 1.1:1); the focus is carried by the darker border (`#475467` vs the unfocused `#868c96`, 7.2:1 on `#fcfcfd`). If a stronger cue is wanted, raise the ring alpha in `dp-form-focus-ring` only.
- **NOT VERIFIED:** Blocks error state in the live flow (non-persistent DOM test only), a disabled control, edit-account page and 768 px on Checkout / My Account / guest forms in this pass (the computed results of `e87931e` for those pages used the same selectors), Quick Order inquiry modal.

## 2026-11-03 (global form focus) - monochrome focus ring on all form controls, VERIFIED ON STAGING

Code `e87931e`, `a49c384` (`sass/theme/_mixins.scss`, `sass/components/_content.scss`, `sass/pages/checkout.scss` + generated `style.css`, `css/pages/checkout.css`). Staging only.

- **Cause of the blue ring:** one token, `$dp-color-focus-ring-primary` (`#c7d7fe`), used as `0 0 0 3px` ring in five rules: the legacy `.custom-form` inputs, the `div.custom-form:not(.subscription-form)` controls (login, register, lost password, My Account, addresses, CF7), the Select2 container focus/open state, the Select2 dropdown search field, and the WooCommerce Blocks checkout text inputs / country-state selects (`checkout.scss`).
- **Fix (shared, no per-page overrides):** new mixin `dp-focus-ring` in `_mixins.scss` = `0 0 0 2px $dp-color-bg-primary, 0 0 0 4px $dp-color-text-primary` (white gap + black ring; the same look the buttons already had, which now reuse the mixin - output identical). All five blue rules include it. The token itself is kept in `_vars.scss` but is no longer used for focus (0 blue rules left in `style.css` and `checkout.css`; `woocommerce.css` never had one). The Contact-only Select2 focus/outline/invalid rules from `f18b804`..`eee864b` became redundant and were folded into the shared Select2 rules; only the Contact option-state block (`$contact-select2-selected-bg: #171717`) remains Contact-specific.
- **State priority:** invalid Select2 (`.woocommerce-invalid` and CF7 `.wpcf7-not-valid + .select2-container`) keeps the red border/ring while focused or open (`#page div.custom-form:not(.subscription-form)` selectors, one `:not()` more specific than the focus rule); invalid text inputs were already red via `[aria-invalid="true"]` / `.wpcf7-not-valid` / `.woocommerce-invalid` placed after the focus rule; Blocks `.has-error` selects now also get the red ring when focused (`checkout.scss`; Blocks sets `aria-invalid` on invalid text inputs, which the `custom-form` rule already turns red); `:disabled` keeps its muted look (rules unchanged). A single inner UA outline on the focused `.select2-selection` is reset on all `custom-form` Select2 controls (the container carries the ring).
- **Exception (unchanged on purpose):** the header search input has its own neutral focus (`1px #141414` + 0.5 px ring, not blue); the cart coupon / newsletter / Quick Order search controls were not touched (none used the blue token). Native `<select>` option lists are not styled (browser-owned); only the closed controls get the ring.
- **Verified on staging (real browser, computed styles):** every tested focus shows border `rgb(13, 18, 28)` + `rgb(255,255,255) 0 0 0 2px, rgb(13,18,28) 0 0 0 4px`, outline none, no double ring, no clipping seen in screenshots, no horizontal overflow (docW = viewport minus scrollbar at 1440/768/390). Contact 1440/768/390: textarea, Select2 (focus/open/invalid+focus/option states); Checkout 1440/768 (Blocks): email, first/last name, address, country and state selects, real Tab order (Ime -> Prezime -> Drzava -> Ulica) with `:focus-visible`; My Account 1440: edit-account (8 fields) and billing address (10 fields incl. Select2 country/state and the ERP read-only fields); guest login, lost-password and registration (`/my-account/?action=register`, 10 fields) at 1440 and 390 in an isolated browser context. Scan of all loaded stylesheets on Contact (768) and Checkout (768): 0 rules containing `#c7d7fe`.
- **NOT VERIFIED:** Blocks field error state in the live Blocks flow (the Blocks UI shows errors only after an order attempt; tested non-persistently by adding `has-error` / `aria-invalid` in the DOM), a disabled control (none exists on the tested pages), Blocks 390 px focus (checked at 1440/768 only), the shipping-address fields of My Account (billing tested; same template), other pages that use `custom-form` (Quick Order inquiry modal, product inquiry) were not opened.

## 2026-11-03 (Select2) - Contact inquiry select enhanced; Checkout deliberately NOT enhanced

Code `ed32d8b`, `6c74e99`, `8fbd425` (`js/select2-init.js`, `sass/components/_content.scss` + generated `style.css`, `functions.php`). Staging only.

- **Contact - root cause:** the theme already ships Select2 4.1.0-rc.0 (`js/select2.min.js`, `css/src/select2.min.css`, gated by `dreampoint_b2b_needs_select2()`, which includes `contact.php`), but `select2-init.js` only matched `.form-group select`, `.sort-area select` and `.variations select`. The Contact Form 7 select (`vrsta-upita`) sits in `.wpcf7-form-control-wrap`, so it was never initialised.
- **Contact - change:** `select2-init.js` now also initialises `.wpcf7 select.wpcf7-select` through the existing `initSelect2()` (duplicate-init guard kept): `width: 100%`, the empty first option ("Odaberi") becomes the placeholder, field name/values/required validation stay on the native select (CF7 lifecycle untouched); a `wpcf7reset` listener re-syncs Select2 after CF7 resets the form. No second Select2 library, no CDN.
- **Styling:** the closed control reuses the existing `#page div.custom-form .select2-container` rules (44 px, radius 6, `#fcfcfd`, `#868c96`, focus ring) - it already matched the Contact inputs. The dropdown panel rules (previously `body.woocommerce-account` only) now apply through `:is(body.woocommerce-account, body.page-template-contact-php)`; Contact-only additions: CF7 invalid state (`.wpcf7-not-valid + .select2-container`, red border/ring), panel `z-index: 10000` (above the 9999 sticky header / mobile bar; Select2's default 1051 opens behind them), and the highlighted row is no longer bold (Select2 sets `aria-selected="true"` on the highlighted row, which made a hover look like a selection; `--selected` rows keep 600). `dreampoint_b2b_select2_admin_bar_fix()` now also runs on `contact.php`: with the admin bar showing the panel was offset by 32/46 px and on mobile covered the control, so the first option was picked on mouseup (administrators only; verified that without the admin bar the panel sits 4 px below the control).
- **Checkout - NOT enhanced (architecture):** checkout is the WooCommerce Blocks checkout. Its country/state fields are React-managed `wc-blocks-components-select` selects (`#shipping-country`, `#shipping-state`, `#billing-*`; 0 Select2 instances, `select2-init.js` matches nothing there); classic `form.woocommerce-checkout` does not exist, so SelectWoo's classic initialisation never runs and attaching Select2/SelectWoo to React-owned DOM would be overwritten on every re-render and conflict with the Store API data flow. Not forced. They are already visually aligned: 44 px, `1px #868c96`, radius 6, `#fcfcfd`, Montserrat 14 px, identical to the Blocks text inputs. Smallest supported alternative if the owner wants a different look: native-select CSS only (scoped `.wc-blocks-components-select__select`), no JS. Side note (not changed): `select2.min.js` / `select2-init.js` are still enqueued on checkout/cart although the Blocks checkout does not use them.
- **Monochrome correction (`f18b804`..`eee864b`, 2026-11-03):** the shared `$dp-color-focus-ring-primary` (`#c7d7fe`, a blue tint) was the only blue on the Contact Select2 (the open/focus ring); option backgrounds were already neutral. On Contact only: focus/open = the project's two-tone keyboard ring (`0 0 0 2px #fff, 0 0 0 4px #0d121c`, black border) and the redundant inner UA outline of `.select2-selection:focus-visible` is off; panel white; normal option white/dark text, highlighted `#f3f4f6`, selected `#171717` + white (owner-specified value, not a token; `$contact-select2-selected-bg` in `_content.scss`), selected+highlighted keeps the dark fill plus an inset `#868c96` ring; disabled keeps the shared muted rule (no disabled option exists on Contact); the CF7 red invalid state also wins while focused/open. Scoped by `body.page-template-contact-php` (panel rules `(0,6,1)` beat the shared `!important` rules `(0,4,1)`; the panel background `(0,3,1)` beats the shared `(0,2,1)`). The account-page Select2 and the Blocks checkout are untouched. Scan of every Select2 element on Contact: no blue colour left. Note: Select2 4.1 changes the value on Arrow Up/Down while CLOSED and focused (library behaviour, unchanged).
- **Verified on staging (admin session):** Contact 1440/768/390: initialised once (no duplicate container/handlers), 654/690/343 px wide x 44 px, opens/closes, mouse click, keyboard (Enter/Space open, Arrow + Enter select, Escape closes and keeps the value and focus), native select value synced, placeholder colour, required validation (empty submit: both fields invalid, CF7 `invalid` status, red Select2 border + CF7 tips, nothing sent), no horizontal overflow, no console errors.
- **NOT VERIFIED:** a real CF7 submission/e-mail (not sent on purpose), `wpcf7reset` re-sync after a successful send (needs a real send), touch input on a real device (mouse/keyboard only in the headless browser), the real non-admin view (simulated by removing the admin bar), Checkout behaviour (unchanged, no Select2 involved).

## 2026-11-03 (E2 FAQ) - FAQ page COMPLETE, VISUAL/UI VERIFIED ON STAGING

Figma CON-02 `79:22758` in file `FcZ1ja4QTalgsMB1vKGOWH` (NOT in the DreamPoint B2B Copy file `Te4kr3o8kXDgBLzHewKSYU`). Code `b06baad`, `07bb7cb`, `212b5e7`, `99559de` (`faq.php`, `inc/faq.php`, `header.php`, `functions.php`, `js/faq.js`, `sass/pages/faq.scss` + `css/pages/faq.css`, four Figma SVGs `img/ico/faq-*.svg`).

- **Owner decisions (2026-11-03):** the three existing `faq-category` terms (Naručivanje, Dostava, Plaćanje) become the tabs; the Figma subtitle is added in Croatian (SUPERSEDED the same day: the text is editor-managed in ACF `intro_text_faq`, no hardcoded copy - see the E2 / D5 follow-up section); existing questions/answers are preserved exactly (no Figma placeholder content, no new terms/posts/ACF content); single-open accordion with the first question expanded; the title stays `Često postavljena pitanja` (breadcrumb unchanged).
- **Tabs:** server-side filtering via `?faq_cat=<slug>` (`inc/faq.php`: `dreampoint_b2b_faq_get_terms()` orders by `term_id` = creation order = owner order, no hardcoded IDs; the first term is the default and uses the clean page URL; an unknown slug falls back to the first term). Rendered as links with `aria-current="page"` inside the `.inner-heading` banner (`header.php`), not as an ARIA tab widget, because each tab is a real page state. The same `tax_query` feeds the list, `max_num_pages` and pagination (`base` carries `faq_cat`), so counts and pages cannot diverge. A page number beyond the last page falls back to the last page (WP_Query reports `max_num_pages = 0` for an empty page, so page 1 is probed first).
- **Subtitle:** SUPERSEDED. The first version fell back to a hardcoded Croatian default; it now reads only the FAQ's own ACF field `intro_text_faq` with no fallback (see the E2 / D5 follow-up section). Existing `.intro-text` style = Montserrat 500 16/24, 380 px.
- **Accordion:** `<h2><button aria-expanded aria-controls>` + `[hidden]` panel; the first item is rendered expanded by PHP; `js/faq.js` is vanilla (jQuery dependency and the dead `initFaqTabs()` removed), one delegated listener, single-open. The slide animation of the old jQuery version is gone (state change is instant). Styling follows Figma: Petrona 600 16 sentence case, answers Montserrat 400 14/18, closed `#fcfcfd`, open `#f3f4f6` with the Figma `#eddef0` top border and 32/16 padding, 16 px gaps, 891 px left-aligned list, 24 px plus/minus (Figma SVGs, mask-recoloured).
- **Gotchas found during QA:** (1) the global `button:focus` / `a:focus { outline: none !important }` swallows outlines, so focus rings are inset/two-tone `box-shadow` (same project convention as header/footer); (2) the FAQ script was versioned by the shared `_S_VERSION`, so browsers kept the old jQuery `faq.js` against the new markup; it is now versioned by `dreampoint_b2b_asset_ver('js/faq.js')` like the CSS.
- **Verified on staging (admin session, 1440/768/390):** 3 tabs; each tab shows exactly its 3 existing questions (titles identical to the audit); first item open on every tab; Enter and Space toggle, opening another closes the previous, `aria-expanded` and `hidden` stay in sync, no duplicate IDs; visible focus ring; no horizontal overflow at 768/390; a long question wraps without touching the icon; header height 320 vs Figma 319, list gap 32, list 891 px at x=120, open card 141 vs 140 px; JSON-LD lists only the visible questions; no new console errors.
- **NOT VERIFIED / limits:** pagination with real data (each category has 3 posts, page size stays 10; no posts were created). Its styling was checked only by injecting the paginate_links markup into the DOM (non-persistent): 40 px items, 8 px-radius dark current page, 20 px arrows and the `#eaecf0` rule match Figma, but live link generation (`faq_cat` + `faq_page`) is verified by code reading and the out-of-range fallback only. No hover state is specified in Figma and none was added. No tablet/mobile Figma frame exists; 768/390 follow the DreamPoint responsive patterns. MPR-05 `11148:38145` (orders empty) uses the same composition as MPR-02 (emoji-sad 144 px, Petrona 600 40 px heading, centred 544 px column), so both templates now share the `.not-found-holder--account` modifier (`sass/pages/myaccount.scss`); the Orders template got the approved `img/ico/emoji-sad.svg`. Orders copy ("Nema povijesti narudžbi", description, "Posjeti trgovinu" -> shop) and Address copy/CTA (shipping editor) are unchanged. Populated orders/addresses untouched (admin check: 2 address cards, 1 order row).

**Verified (staging, `vis_none` via User Switching, switched back to admin; nothing saved):** orders and addresses empty states at 1440 px (icon 144, heading 40 px/600 sentence case, 544 px column, 48 px button) and 390 px (no overflow), console clean. Open/minor: Figma MPR-05 orders button looks ~160 px wide (16 px label); staging keeps the XL 18 px label (195 px) - not changed. For `vis_none` WooCommerce renders its native "Potvrdite adresu e-pošte" notice above the Orders empty state, which pushes it down versus Figma (not theme code).

**Address eligibility (read-only, counts only, 2026-11-03):** the empty-address component renders only when BOTH `billing_address_1` and `shipping_address_1` are empty (`my-address.php`). B2B registration requires billing address/city/postcode/country/phone/company and copies billing -> shipping (`inc/b2b-registration.php`); `b2b-partner-importer` writes both. No theme code populates addresses on APROS approval (`inc/registration-approval.php` has no address handling), so APROS does not guarantee addresses. Addressless accounts are therefore possible only for accounts created outside the registration/importer paths (admin-created/test users). Staging: 9 users, 5 with both, 1 billing-only, 0 shipping-only, 3 with neither (`vis_none`, `vis_offer`, `vis_rule_brand`, all test customers). Billing master fields are protected for existing accounts (`dreampoint_b2b_billing_master_applies()`; empty stored values stay empty), so an addressless customer can only fill shipping from My Account, which is why the CTA opens the shipping editor.

## 2026-10-29 — DreamPoint B2B T7.1/T7.2 and Checkout Functional Audit — EOD Handoff

Documentation-only handoff. No code, staging, database, API or Git history was changed by it. Detail lives in `docs/active/figma-alignment.md` §53–53.6 and in the earlier 2026-10-29 updates below; this section is the entry point for the next session. Nothing here is an approval: every "recommended" item needs explicit owner authorization first.

### Completed today
- **T7 My Account visual alignment:** commit `227d59b` (local only, NOT pushed; `master` is one commit ahead of `origin/master`). Runtime files deployed to staging with verified SHA-256 hashes (backup + rollback: `/home/dreampoint.b2b/backup-recovery/t7-20261029-142952/`, rollback NOT executed). Technical QA passed with limits (no screenshots through the extension provider). Functional QA (real saves) still PENDING. Record: §53, §53.1, §53.2.
- **Read-only audits (no mutation):** T7.1 profile/phone ownership audit; billing identity and address enforcement audit (my-account, address endpoints, classic checkout, Store API); Blocks checkout / Store API billing data-flow audit; checkout functional completeness audit.

### Uncommitted local work (9 items, branch `master`, HEAD `227d59b`)
`css/pages/myaccount.css`, `sass/pages/myaccount.scss`, `header.php`, `inc/b2b-registration.php`, `inc/checkout-logic.php`, `inc/my-account.php`, `woocommerce/myaccount/user-form.php`, `docs/active/figma-alignment.md`, `docs/active/status.md` (paths relative to `themes/dreampoint-b2b/`). Do not reset, clean or stash them.
- **T7.1 — LOCAL, UNCOMMITTED, UNDEPLOYED** (§53.3, §53.4): intro reuses `.intro-text`; display-name helper uses `text-sm-medium`; profile = read-only company card -> "Podaci o računu" (first name, last name, display name, e-mail, phone) -> "Lozinka" -> one "Spremi izmjenu"; company name and OIB are customer read-only (renderer and saver removed, registration/admin/importer untouched); duplicate `account_phone` consolidated into one renderer, one validator on `woocommerce_save_account_details_errors` (required, format `^[0-9+\s\-\(\)]+$`, runs before any write) and one writer. Verified by isolated tests and local browser only; no real account save was performed.
- **T7.2 — PARTIAL, STORE API OPEN** (§53.5): the seven billing master fields (`billing_company`, `billing_address_1/2`, `billing_city`, `billing_postcode`, `billing_state`, `billing_country`) are protected on the My Account billing endpoint (per-key `woocommerce_process_myaccount_field_*` filters, UI locked) and on the classic checkout (pre-save `woocommerce_checkout_update_customer`; the former priority-999 protection was a no-op because it ran after `$customer->save()`). Shipping, phone and e-mail unchanged by design. 25/25 isolated tests PASS; browser checks at 1440 and 390 passed; NO runtime save tests were run. **Store API `cart/update-customer` and Blocks checkout are NOT protected.**

### Staging state (`dreampoint.b2b.uncledev.cloud`; no production DreamPoint B2B exists)
- Runs the T7 runtime files only (15 files deployed outside git). T7.1/T7.2 are NOT deployed. Staging git HEAD `320b46d` shows the 7 deployed PHP/CSS files as modified and the 8 `img/ico/account-*.svg` as untracked: reconcile before any future `git pull` (owner approval needed).
- Config observed read-only (snapshot before 2026-11-02; payment/shipping since reconfigured, see ADR-037): Blocks checkout page ID 12 "Blagajna"; gateways BACS + COD enabled (cheque off); shipping zone Croatia = free shipping + flat rate (no local pickup, although the checkout block mentions pickup); `woocommerce_hold_stock_minutes=60` (native, unpaid orders only); `DP_BYPASS_APPROVAL=true`; guest checkout option on but the portal gate blocks anonymous access; Reserved Stock Pro is NOT installed.

### Verified evidence (do not re-derive)
- Blocks checkout is the active checkout; the classic form is not on the page (`wc-ajax=checkout` exists but needs a classic nonce).
- Existing runtime evidence: order #23358 (Apros sandbox, delivery location 2+ branch, ERP 4244, new-order reset confirmed), #23407 (ERP 4345), #23408 (BACS, on-hold). Guest/cart/checkout guards verified by real HTTP on 2026-10-08.
- Store API order of operations (WooCommerce 11.2.1): `update_customer_from_request` (customer `save()`) -> draft order built from the session customer (`update_addresses_from_cart`) -> `update_order_from_request` (no billing) -> `sync_customer_data_with_order` (fresh customer from order data, `save()`). `woocommerce_checkout_update_user_meta` is never fired by the Store API. The order billing address therefore comes from the customer object, and protecting that object changes new-order billing snapshots.
- Client: initial `useShippingAsBilling` is derived from "stored billing equals stored shipping"; when true, shipping edits are copied into billing. The billing form is hidden by CSS only (`#billing-fields`). The actual POST payload is NOT runtime verified.
- APROS exporter `uncle-dev-importer/order.php` (staging, read-only): partner from `apros_partner_code` of the order user; billing address, phone, e-mail and `billingTitle` (the person's name) from the WooCommerce order; `billingOib` is sent empty; company and OIB are not exported; delivery location from `_apros_delivery_location_id` with fallback to the partner's first location; triggers on `processing`, `thankyou` (cod/bacs/cheque) and `payment_complete`; idempotent through `_erp_sync_status` and the "already imported" response; failures are written to order notes and the same meta key, which blocks automatic re-sends; only the manual order action `sync_to_erp` retries. Successful sandbox exports exist, so the exporter is not generally broken. Automatic retry must not be added without verifying idempotency and duplicate-order safety with Apros.

### Checkout status
- **Functional: PARTIAL.** Main flow proven; gaps: Store API billing master protection; no reliable ERP failure retry or notification; one-hour cart-level stock reservation not implemented (mandatory per ADR-007); payment model: BACS only is CONFIRMED (ADR-037, supersedes this earlier "unconfirmed" note; COD disabled on staging 2026-11-02); shipping/delivery UX and the Silkypress checkbox ("Koristite istu adresu za dostavu" only hides `#shipping-fields`) unclear; stale payment rules (`gls`, `pickup_location`, stripe/paypal) in `inc/checkout-logic.php`; MOQ/MPC/multipack missing (ADR-035); order confirmation, order numbering and order e-mails not QA'd.
- **Visual: INCOMPLETE.** Figma CH-01/02/03 card structure not implemented (only T1 heading/label fixes). Not a functional requirement by itself: VAT number and contact person have no data source.

### OPEN decisions (none approved; business decisions, not defects)
1. Store API Option A — new orders use the authoritative master billing identity/address (recommendation only).
2. Payment model — RESOLVED 2026-11-02: BACS only (ADR-037); COD disabled on staging. Shipping methods are INTERIM, client approval pending (ADR-037).
3. Shipping-address cards / "add new address" and delivery-location behaviour — pending APROS clarification. Verified 2026-11-02 (ADR-037 addendum): 2+ location UI behaves per ADR-010; 1-location and post-order reset NOT VERIFIED. Shipping methods (GLS, Osobna dostava – područje Zagreba, Kupac sam preuzima robu) are INTERIM — owner-authorized, client approval pending; A11 mapping limitation and the deferred APROS inquiry are recorded in ADR-037.
4. ERP export failure notification and a safe retry policy.
5. Order number format; source for the VAT-number and contact-person fields. [RESOLVED: native numbering retained 2026-11-27, ADR-050; VAT / contact person resolved 2026-11-25.]
6. Meaning and behaviour of the Silkypress "Koristite istu adresu za dostavu" checkbox.
7. MOQ/MPC/multipack rules still awaiting approval.
Established and NOT reopened: company, OIB and master billing address are customer read-only; shipping address and contact phone are editable.

### Recommended next-session order (recommendation, not authorization)
1. Review this handoff; decide Option A. 2. Only after approval, finish the local T7.2 Store API protection (hooks `woocommerce_store_api_cart_update_customer_from_request` and `woocommerce_store_api_checkout_update_customer_from_request`). 3. Review ERP failure visibility and safe retry. 4. Settle the payment and shipping decisions. 5. Plan stock reservation. 6. Finish the remaining checkout functional work. 7. ## Update 2026-10-19 (product reviews) — REVIEW-TAB FOLLOW-UP RECLASSIFIED: B (NOT SPECIFIED IN FIGMA / NO ACTION REQUIRED)

Read-only investigation (Figma MCP + theme code + existing docs; no code, staging or settings changed; Phase 4 closeout above is unchanged).

- **Figma:** PDP-01/PDP-02 (`11148:32726`, `11148:32859`, file `Te4kr3o8kXDgBLzHewKSYU`) have a tab bar with `Opis / Specifikacije / Sadržaj paketa / Dokumenti` (plus an empty fifth slot). No Recenzije/Reviews tab, rating stars, review count or review form on the PDP; the Product card (`11148:32832`) has no stars either. Absent from the approved design, not explicitly prohibited anywhere in the docs.
- **Implementation:** `woocommerce/content-single-product.php:332` wraps `woocommerce_output_product_data_tabs()` in `<div class="wc-tabs-section block" style="display: none;">` (present since the monorepo migration `e90017a`; already recorded as a pre-existing known item in `docs/decisions.md` ADR-014, "mobile tabs dropdown"). So the ENTIRE tabs section (Description, Additional information, Reviews) is hidden on every PDP; the zero-size bounds seen during Phase 4 QA are this inline style, not a review-specific fault. WooCommerce review markup is still emitted (the real `.woocommerce-noreviews` notice was observed). No theme code removes reviews; no document requires them.
- **Conclusion:** hidden review UI is not a regression and not an actionable Figma mismatch. The historical QA observation (zero-size panel, stars not visible) stays valid as a record. If product tabs are later implemented per Figma (Opis/Specifikacije/Sadržaj paketa/Dokumenti), that is a separate PDP task and the owner must decide then whether reviews are wanted at all (WooCommerce setting, not decided here).
- Review-star colours in Phase 4 remain "no Figma star evidence; existing-token fallback" (§41); that is unchanged.

## Update 2026-10-16 (catalog menu FOUC) — CATALOG MENU FOUC FIXED AND VERIFIED (staging `299770a`)

Root cause (measured on staging, rAF sampling): `header.php` renders `#catalog-menu` as a direct child of the header `<nav>`, but every hide/position rule is scoped to `#header #main-header ul.links li .catalog-menu`. Until the deferred `js/mobile-menu.js` moves the panel into `li.cat-toggler` on DOMContentLoaded, it was `position:static; visibility:visible` (132x706 px, ~340-420 ms) and then snapped hidden. Fix: one pre-move rule `#header #main-header nav > .catalog-menu { position:absolute; visibility:hidden; opacity:0; pointer-events:none }` in `sass/components/_catalog-menu.scss` (+ compiled `style.css`); no PHP/JS change. Verified at 1440/768/390 with `mobile-menu.js` delayed 2 s: 0 visible frames; hover, focus-within, Tab into panel, Escape, `aria-expanded`, no tabbable links while closed, no console errors, header height/trigger position unchanged. Known limitation: the existing 0.15 s visibility transition means focus opens the panel after ~150 ms (pre-existing). Localhost not used.

## Update 2026-10-09 — Product card, button capabilities and Toastify refinement — staging PASS; simple-product AJAX add-to-cart E2E PASS

Deployed to staging and validated read-only (staging HEAD `34c80a1`): button capabilities `.button--s`, `.button--destructive`, `.button--icon-only` (`a79f643`); simple-product card action migrated to `button button--outline button--sm button--icon-only` with `icon-cart` (`f3438a8`); product-card action overlay fix (`5430547`, `.action-holder` positioned + `pointer-events`); Toastify refinement (`34c80a1`: Croatian success text, localized close label, mobile toast lifted 15px above the 73px fixed toolbar). Status: product-card visual and overlay QA PASS (1440/390/320); Toastify UI and responsive QA PASS (1440/390/360/320, synthetic notifications). **Simple-product AJAX add-to-cart E2E: PASS on staging (2026-10-09).** Product 7897 (SKU P-53466), authorized staging admin session, one real Playwright locator click (`isTrusted: true`, viewport 929px), in a context where the tab was visible and focused. Directly observed (instrumentation + native network recording, not inferred from cart state): exactly one `wc-ajax=add_to_cart` POST (200, `quantity=1&product_id=7897`, response `fragments` + `cart_hash`, no error); one `adding_to_cart` and one `added_to_cart` event; button `loading` then `added`; exactly one success toast (`Proizvod dodan u košaricu!`, close label `Zatvori obavijest`, ~3.4s lifetime); fragments refreshed, counter 5 to 6, mini-cart opened ~100ms after the toast; no navigation, no JS errors. Server-backed re-read confirmed one new cart line; only that line was then removed through the native WooCommerce remove link and the baseline (three original lines, total 5, counter 5) was verified restored. History: two earlier attempts were PARTIAL (instrumentation lost when the Playwright MCP connection dropped; the add and a targeted rollback were verified server-side only) and BLOCKED (hidden Chrome tab stalled locator clicks) before this run. Not covered by this E2E and still unvalidated: partner approval / bucket guard negative paths; variable-product and out-of-stock card branches (functionally); Quick Order AJAX integration; the mobile AJAX lifecycle (earlier mobile Toastify UI QA stands); deferred items: PDP max-quantity toast close label, dormant `.is-danger` styling. This does not validate all WooCommerce cart scenarios. Variable and out-of-stock card actions are intentionally unchanged (no Figma card state). Deferred: PDP max-quantity toast keeps the English close label; dormant `.is-danger` conflicts with the Toastify `!important` background; toast offset assumes the 73px toolbar (`$dp-mobile-toolbar-height`). ## Update 2026-10-06 — Slice 5: row-level cart/submit error UX (staging-accepted, plugin v1.0.29)

`/cart/sync` returns a normalized, non-sensitive failure contract (`failed` + `out_of_stock` / `quantity_unavailable` / `product_unavailable` / `not_addable`, one result per submitted item, no stock figure); variation ownership is verified before stock is read, hidden/nonexistent/mismatched items are indistinguishable, and an existing cart line respects current stock. The client shows the reason on the affected row (`aria-invalid` / `aria-describedby`) and a polite footer summary instead of `alert()`; an unusable response (network/HTTP/timeout) is an *ambiguous* state with its own message and no auto-retry. Commits `5c07c53`, `0aee6f1`. Record: `docs/decisions.md` ADR-016; `docs/frozen/quick-order-local-state-architecture.md` Addendum 2026-10-06 (Slice 5). Residual risk: a manual retry after a lost-but-applied response would add twice (no idempotency added).

Remaining QO scope (not scheduled): Excel Import; mobile QO design; WBW drops `orderby` when a filter is applied (pre-existing); theme brand `get_terms` leak outside Quick Order.

## Milestone: COMPLETE (2026-07-21)

Quick Order has completed its planned development cycle and has entered
**maintenance mode**. All systems tracked in this document are implemented,
staging-deployed, and end-to-end validated as of commit `2d5c00a`. No open
implementation work remains for this feature. Future changes are
enhancements against a stable baseline, not completion of pending scope —
see `docs/active/current-phase.md` and the plugin's `readme.md` (theme-root
`wp-content/plugins/dp-b2b-quick-order/readme.md`) for the authoritative
current-state description.

**Release status:**

| Stage | Status |
|-------|--------|
| Local implementation | COMPLETE |
| Staging deployment | COMPLETE (latest: commit `2d5c00a`, 2026-07-21) |
| Staging validation | COMPLETE (incl. variable products/variations — see synthetic catalog generator run, 2026-07-10; catalog filters + WBW AJAX lifecycle + local-state + additive cart sync — see 2026-07-21 validation below) |
| Release candidate | READY |
| Production deployment | NOT APPLICABLE — no production environment provisioned for this project. `dreampoint.b2b.uncledev.cloud` (`dream9399`) is currently the only deployment target. Do not treat staging as production. |

---

**Architecture note (2026-07-10):** Quick Order has transitioned from the
real-time CartSync model to the local-state workspace model. See
`docs/frozen/quick-order-local-state-architecture.md` (canonical, current) and
the Supersession Note in `docs/frozen/quick-order-sync-architecture.md`
(historical). Verified locally via Playwright (vis_full test user): zero
network requests on quantity change, pagination-persistent local state,
single chunked submit, partial-failure rows retained, keyboard navigation,
visible focus indicators on new controls. **Staging-verified 2026-07-10**
(`dreampoint.b2b.uncledev.cloud`, commit `e7b98ab`): >50-row batch chunking
(51 items → 2 sequential requests, 50+1), mini-cart fragment refresh +
Toastify, cart icon counter increment, cache-busting (bumped
`DP_QUICK_ORDER_VERSION` 1.0.1→1.0.2 after finding 7-day browser cache with a
static version string), full golden-path E2E.

**Variable-product staging validation (2026-07-10, commit `3e9dc1c`):** staging
catalog has no real variable products, so the existing synthetic catalog
generator (`docs/historical/synthetic-b2b-catalog.md`) was run on staging
(taxonomies + 200 simple + 10 variable/183 variations) to validate variable-
product rendering, independent variation rows/qty controls, local subtotal,
bulk add-to-cart, and WC cart contents end-to-end — including an
organically-triggered partial-failure/stock-guard path (one row exceeded
available stock, correctly rejected and retained locally while the rest of
the batch synced). Catalog reset (`wp dp-b2b reset-catalog`) and cart cleanup
ran afterward; staging was back to its pre-test state (6 original products)
**as of 2026-07-10 — superseded, see policy note below.**
Release candidate is READY. **Production deployment is NOT APPLICABLE** — no
production environment is provisioned for this project; `dreampoint.b2b.uncledev.cloud`
remains the only deployment target.

**Staging dataset policy (2026-07-13, supersedes the reset described above):**
the generated synthetic catalog is now a **persistent development dataset**,
not a disposable per-test fixture. Staging currently carries the full
generated set (216 products / 183 variations / 42 categories / 34 brands,
plus the 6 original products) via `wp dp-b2b generate-catalog` (all three
phases). **Do not run `wp dp-b2b reset-catalog` on staging** except (a) after
ERP import becomes available, or (b) on explicit user instruction. Routine
Quick Order development and regression testing should use this dataset as-is;
only remove ad-hoc cart contents/test orders created during a session, never
the catalog itself. See `docs/historical/synthetic-b2b-catalog.md` for
generator mechanics.

**Superseded 2026-09-02 — synthetic PRODUCTS removed from staging (ERP import
is now live):** the `2026-07-13` "do not reset" condition (a) has been met.
The Apros ERP import (`wp-content/plugins/uncle-dev-importer`) populated staging
with real product data on 2026-08-19, so the synthetic products were removed
via `wp dp-b2b reset-catalog --batch=20260713_1138` — **210 parent products +
183 variations** deleted. The `[DEV]` product categories (24) and brands (30)
and all Brand Fixtures were **retained** (batch mode never touches terms).
Staging's product catalog is now the ERP dataset. Full record + the
`recalcMetaValues()` WBW orphan-row cleanup that followed:
`docs/decisions.md` ADR-006.

**Staging validation — 2026-07-21 (commit `2d5c00a`):** full Playwright
regression against `dreampoint.b2b.uncledev.cloud/quick-order/` after
deploying the WBW AJAX container-check placeholder fix and configuring
WBW's "Product List / Loader Selector" (admin, filter views 2 and 3) to
`.dp-qo-wbw-ajax-placeholder`. Confirmed: Brand/Sort By/In Stock/native
Clear all proceed via AJAX with zero full-page reloads;  `wpfAjaxSuccess`
fires on every WBW-driven change; Quick Order's own REST endpoint
(`/wp-json/dreampoint-b2b/v1/quick-order/products`) re-fetches and re-renders
on each change; the placeholder itself stays hidden throughout; the visible
table is exclusively Quick Order's own `table.dp-qo-table` (WBW never
renders a competing product list); local quantity state and the footer
subtotal survive a WBW-triggered re-render even when the edited row scrolls
off the newly-sorted/filtered result set; Add to Cart posts to
`/quick-order/cart/sync` and is reflected in the native WooCommerce mini-cart
count; `.is-added` and footer totals update/reset correctly around the
cart-sync round trip; browser Back/Forward triggers a full document reload
(this is WBW's own documented `popstate` → `location.reload()` behavior,
already recorded in `docs/frozen/quick-order-local-state-architecture.md`
§11's 2026-07-13 revision note — not a regression introduced by this fix);
zero console errors/warnings and zero failed network requests throughout.

| System | Status | Stable | Staging Ready | Notes |
|--------|--------|--------|---------------|-------|
| CartSync (debounce, token, abort) | SUPERSEDED | — | — | Real-time per-keystroke cart writes removed. See `docs/frozen/quick-order-local-state-architecture.md`. |
| Local Quick Order state + explicit submit | ACTIVE | Yes | Yes | See `docs/frozen/quick-order-local-state-architecture.md` §2–§4. Staging-validated 2026-07-21 (quantity state and footer subtotal survive a WBW-triggered re-render; deferred submit confirmed via Add to Cart round trip). |
| Pagination | ACTIVE | Yes | Yes | In-place re-render. Quantities re-hydrate from local state across pages instead of resetting — verified via Playwright (set page 1 → page 2 → back to page 1, quantity persisted). See local-state doc §2. |
| Variation handling | ACTIVE | Yes | No | Dropdown removed; each variation is an independent purchasable line, stacked inside its parent's single `.dp-qo-row` using the table's real Naziv/Stanje/Cijena/Kol. columns (no `colspan`, not a sibling top-level row). See local-state doc §6. |
| Visibility integration | ACTIVE | Yes | Yes | Gate fires on `add_to_cart` only. No retroactive revalidation — intentional. Unaffected by the local-state transition (fires at submit time now instead of per keystroke, same gate). |
| Sorting | ACTIVE | Yes | Yes | `qo_orderby` / `qo_order` params. Title and price sort, ASC/DESC toggle. Isolated from WOOF `orderby` detection. |
| Filter integration (WOOF/WBW) | ACTIVE | Yes | Yes | Category, brand, price range, and pa_* attribute filters via REST — all three taxonomy-based filters (category/brand/attributes) now share one parsing pipeline driven by WBW's own `data-taxonomy`/`data-get-attribute`/`data-query-logic` DOM metadata, no hardcoded param-name/delimiter assumptions (2026-07-13 hardening — see `docs/frozen/quick-order-local-state-architecture.md` §11 doctrine). Filter-change detection via WBW's native `wpfAjaxSuccess` event (was a `history.pushState` wrapper). Isolation guard strips wpf_query from QO WP_Query instances. WBW's own AJAX success handler previously fell back to a full `location.reload()` on this page (no `ul.products` container to satisfy its own container check) — fixed 2026-07-21 by pointing WBW's "Product List / Loader Selector" setting at a hidden `.dp-qo-wbw-ajax-placeholder` element (commit `2d5c00a`); see frozen doc's 2026-07-21 revision note. |
| Catalog filters (New/Best Seller/Already Ordered/In Stock) | ACTIVE | Yes | Yes | See `docs/active/quick-order-catalog-filters-spec.md` (design record, now historical) and plugin `readme.md` → "Catalog Filters" (current, authoritative). In Stock fully native WBW; other three are Quick Order-owned, `qo_*` URL params. Staging-validated 2026-07-21 (Brand/In Stock/native Clear exercised directly; New/Best Seller/Already Ordered confirmed present and wired via commit history — `35f5730`, `94220ae` — and the synthetic catalog's seeded tiers, see `docs/historical/synthetic-b2b-catalog.md`). |
| Cart totals footer | ACTIVE | Yes | Yes | Local-state subtotal/count footer (local-state doc §3), verified via Playwright to compute correctly (item count, row count, subtotal). No longer reads `data.totals` from a sync response. Staging-validated 2026-07-21: updates on qty change, resets to zero after a successful Add to Cart sync. |
| Variable stock neutral state | ACTIVE | Yes | Yes | Table was stale — this was delivered in the 2026-05-12 V1.1 plan. Neutral badge before variation selection is moot once all variations render as independently purchasable rows grouped under their parent (local-state doc §6), but the badge logic itself already exists. |
| Qty +/- buttons | ACTIVE | Yes | Yes | Table was stale — this was delivered in the 2026-05-12 V1.1 plan (`product-list.js` / `row-sync.js` already implement +/- controls). |
| Admin bypass | ACTIVE | Yes | Yes | Table was stale — this was delivered in the 2026-05-12 V1.1 plan (`manage_woocommerce` bypass in access guard). |
| Cross-page cart hydration | MOOT | — | — | No longer a limitation under the local-state model — state persists across in-page pagination by design (local-state doc §2). Still not persisted across navigation/reload (§5, intentional). |
| Offline / network failure handling | DEFERRED | No | No | Applies to the submit action only now (local-state doc §9) — no retry/queue persistence if a chunked submit fails partway. |
| Performance guards | ACTIVE | Yes | Yes | `CART_SYNC_MAX_BATCH = 50` unchanged server-side; frontend chunks submits over 50 rows sequentially (`cart-submit.js`, local-state doc §4.2) — not yet exercised with a real >50-row selection locally (dev catalog page size is 50, so chunking logic is implemented but chunk-boundary behavior is unverified beyond code review). No object hydration, still true. AbortController/stale-token guards no longer apply — nothing is in-flight to race. |
| Playwright E2E coverage | DEFERRED | No | No | Automated E2E test suite is intentionally out of scope for the current project. Validation is currently performed through repeatable Playwright-driven verification. Performed repeatedly through development, most recently the full 2026-07-21 staging regression above. Deferred item is specifically an automated, repeatable suite file in the repo, not test coverage itself. |

## Status Key

- **ACTIVE** — built, tested locally, works as designed
- **EXPERIMENTAL** — partially built; behavior may be incomplete or untested
- **DEFERRED** — intentionally not built yet; no regression, just missing functionality
