## 2026-11-24 - C6 R2-A (variation selected -> gallery): IMPLEMENTED ON STAGING / PHP PAYLOAD VERIFIED / REAL BROWSER QA PENDING (uncommitted)

- **Correction of the earlier same-day finding** ("no product has variation images"): it queried the native `_thumbnail_id` (0 on all 1291 variations). In DreamPoint B2B variation images are the ACF repeater `product_variation_images` (key `field_68cc4a83c9e47`; sub-fields `variation_sku` + `images[].image`, return format ID) stored on the PARENT product, owned by the importer plugin `uncle-dev-importer` (`acf-json/group_68cc4a812f3fe.json`, `hooks.php`, `Importer::update_variation_images()`). The plugin is NOT in the local `wp-content/plugins`, which is why the local code search never saw it.
- **Staging statistics (read-only):** 200 parent products, 1283 rows (all match a child SKU), 1137 rows with several images, 0 parents sharing an image between variations, 184 parents with >= 2 variations. Examples: `c-sun-baby` (12698, 2 variations 12699/12700, 5 images each), `d-reading` (10278, 48 variations, 5 images each).
- **Architecture:** `hooks.php` appends every ACF image to the parent gallery ids (row order) - so the theme gallery (`content-single-product.php`) already holds all slides with `data-attachment-id`; on the PDP it returns 0 for the native variation image (placeholder branch), elsewhere the first ACF image.
- **Root cause (code + PHP evidence; browser confirmation pending):** `findVariationSlideIndex()` used only `variation.image_id/src`, which is empty on the PDP -> index -1 -> no navigation.
- **Fix:** `dreampoint_b2b_variation_gallery_ids()` (`inc/product-search.php`) adds `dp_gallery_image_ids` (ordered ACF IDs by variation SKU) to `woocommerce_available_variation`; `js/slick-init.js` matches the first ID by `data-attachment-id` (native matching stays as fallback), and ignores a `found_variation` whose `variation_id` differs from the form's current one (stale/rapid switching). R2-B, Slick/Fancybox config, R1-T1 guards untouched.
- **Deploy:** targeted copy of the two files to staging (staging copies were byte-identical to HEAD before), backup `/home/dreampoint.b2b/backups/c6-r2a-20261124-090103`, SHA-256 equal to local, owner `dream9399:dream9399` 664, `php -l` OK. Staging Git untouched; local uncommitted.
- **Verified:** PHP payload for `c-sun-baby` and `d-reading` (IDs and order). **NOT verified:** any browser interaction (the Playwright tab was a guest session; login not done by Claude).
- **Update 2026-11-24 (runtime QA):** first runtime QA found a race: Slick `waitForAnimate: true` drops `slickGoTo` during a fade, so rapid variation changes left a stale image (d-reading: Black, +1, then 3 and 2 without delay showed 10337 instead of 10332). Fix in `js/slick-init.js`: `goToGallerySlide()` keeps the LAST requested slide and applies it after the transition (Slick fires `afterChange` BEFORE clearing `animating`, hence one 0 ms timer), a variation without ACF images clears the pending request, reset goes through the same path. Staging `js/slick-init.js` SHA-256 `688fa126...b54f`; backups under `/home/dreampoint.b2b/backups/c6-r2a-*` (original, v1, v2). Verified (native dropdowns, DOM attachment IDs, console 0 errors): d-reading 10279/10280/10281 -> 10327/10332/10337, failing sequence 6/6, multi-switch, reset variants, c-sun-baby 12699/12700 -> 12701/12706, 390 px, Fancybox opens the current image, fallback variation 15099 (speed-cat-1, no ACF images) shows the default first slide. NOT verified: reliable real mouse clicks (hidden Playwright tab; forced clicks were intermittent), real touch, Fancybox close via Escape. Uncommitted.
- **Owner QA 2026-11-24:** the owner manually tested C6 R2-A on staging and confirmed that all tested variation-to-gallery combinations work. Checkpoint committed locally (not pushed). C6 R2-B (gallery -> dropdowns) approved by the owner the same day.

## 2026-11-24 - C6 R2-B (gallery -> dropdowns): IMPLEMENTED ON STAGING / SYNTHETIC-CLICK QA PASSED / REAL MOUSE NOT VERIFIED (uncommitted; R2-A checkpoint `62438e7`, local only)

- Owner approved bidirectional synchronization after manually confirming R2-A. Staging max = 48 variations per product (< WooCommerce AJAX threshold 100), so every product embeds `product_variations`; no PHP change and no new endpoint were needed.
- **Approach (`js/slick-init.js` only):** on a USER-driven main-gallery `afterChange` the owner variation is found from the embedded `dp_gallery_image_ids` (R2-A payload) and its complete `attributes` are written to the native selects with `.val().trigger('change')`; WooCommerce resolves the variation itself (variation_id, price, stock, qty, add-to-cart state).
- **Loop prevention:** (1) `programmaticSlide` marks navigation issued by `goToGallerySlide()`, its `afterChange` never drives the dropdowns (also covers reset and initial state); (2) `galleryVariationId` + 1.5 s TTL makes the following `found_variation` skip R2-A navigation and the intermediate `reset_data` events skip the reset-to-slide-0; a real dropdown change (`event.originalEvent`) clears it; (3) a user-driven change cancels any pending programmatic request. Same-variation images and images owned by no variation change nothing.
- **Staging:** `js/slick-init.js` SHA-256 `5881b864...663b`, baseline was R2-A `688fa126...`, backup `/home/dreampoint.b2b/backups/c6-r2b-pre-20261124-092146` (R2-A backups kept).
- **Verified (native dropdowns + jQuery-synthetic gallery clicks, DOM IDs, console 0 errors):** d-reading, c-sun-baby, speed-cat-1 (incl. 390 px). NOT verified: real mouse/touch (hidden Playwright tab), keyboard.
- **C6 R2-B intermittent defect (owner QA 2026-11-24, d-reading): FIXED on staging, synthetic-click QA PASSED, owner re-test pending (uncommitted).** Variation 10282 = Blue Tortoise / +2 (SKU 42058, EAN 3760222624381, ACF IDs 10342-10346, slides 15-19). Root cause (reproduced, instrumented): WooCommerce removes every select option not valid with the OTHER current selections; Blue Tortoise exists only with +2 / +2.5, so with Black/+1 selected the Boja select had no `blue-tortoise` option, `.val('blue-tortoise')` failed silently and only +2 was set (vid empty, Color empty); the second visit worked because the option existed by then. Fix (`js/slick-init.js`, `selectVariationFromSlide`): clear the differing selects with one change event (WooCommerce restores all options), then apply the target values. Staging SHA-256 `4fd90518...e916`, baseline R2-B `5881b864...`, backup `/home/dreampoint.b2b/backups/c6-r2b-v1-20261124-093250`. QA: Black/+1 -> BT/+2 10/10, other colour same dioptre, other colour + dioptre, unselected, partial (dioptre only / colour only), sweep of all 48 variations forward and reverse 94/94 with correct selects, variation_id and purchase state (incl. out of stock), same-variation navigation, dropdown -> gallery, rapid, reset (no reselect), speed-cat-1; console 0 errors. Note: the dropdowns update when the gallery transition ends (afterChange, ~1 s after the click). Real mouse/touch NOT verified.

## 2026-11-24 - C6 FINAL CLOSURE: R2-A COMPLETE / VERIFIED, R2-B COMPLETE / VERIFIED

Owner repeated the manual staging QA after the last R2-B correction and accepted the full bidirectional synchronization (scope above, current staging catalog). Effort: about 6 h for all of C6. Final staging `js/slick-init.js` SHA-256 `4fd905188abe7e93821b8841dc0dac62e85414ec7f5c1890df70bc13b891e916`; `inc/product-search.php` `567362d3...b004` (unchanged since R2-A). Summary: variation images = ACF repeater `product_variation_images` (importer plugin) exposed as `dp_gallery_image_ids`; dropdown -> gallery (ID-first match, stale-event guard, last-request-wins because Slick `waitForAnimate` drops `slickGoTo` during a fade); gallery -> dropdowns (`programmaticSlide` + `galleryVariationId` TTL loop guard; clear-then-set because WooCommerce removes options invalid with the other selections); fallback for variations without ACF images = default first slide (WooCommerce `reset_data`). Regression: all 48 `d-reading` variations in both directions (94/94 + 47/47 forward/reverse), owner manual verification. Limits kept: embedded variation data only (AJAX threshold 100, staging max 48); keyboard, touch and Fancybox-after-R2-B not separately verified. Next roadmap task: B6.

Tightest case 320 px: Search box ends 107.5, Quick Order starts 114.8; Cart box starts 246.8, Quick Order ends 239.4 (about 7 px clear). Desktop >= 992: only the Cart is affected (box 44x45, icon unmoved). Keyboard focus ring on these controls is now drawn around the 44 px box (same ring design).

**Mobile menu (`mobile-menu.js`, existing architecture):** opening moves focus to the close control and sets `aria-expanded` on the hamburger (it was static `false` before); entering a submenu stores the trigger and focuses the first visible link of the new panel; Back / Escape returns focus to that trigger; closing restores focus to the hamburger. `inert` is applied to siblings along the path from the menu up to `<body>` (never an ancestor of the menu; only elements this script marked are released, pre-existing `inert` is preserved) and, while a drill-down is open, to every row / control outside the deepest active panel. Escape (document `keydown`, only while the menu is open): nested submenu -> `goBack()`, root -> `closeMenu()`; the search / catalog / brands handlers are untouched. Tab / Shift+Tab wrap inside the menu. Crossing 992 px (matchMedia) with the menu open closes it without moving focus and releases all inert. Opening the menu closes an active mobile search overlay first (mutual exclusivity).

**Focus-visible (`_nav.scss`):** close / Back controls and menu links get the existing two-tone ring (`0 0 0 2px #fff, 0 0 0 4px #0d121c`); `.submenu-toggle` gets an inset ring (an outer ring would be clipped at the right edge of the scrolling list). `:focus-visible` only - mouse / touch appearance unchanged.

**QA (authenticated staging, Playwright):** native `keyboard.press` Tab / Shift+Tab / Enter / Escape at 390 px: open -> focus on close control (ring computed), Shift+Tab wraps to the last link, Tab wraps to the first, Enter on "Katalog proizvoda" toggle -> focus in panel, 2nd level -> focus in deepest panel (5 then 13 inert elements, deepest panel never inert), Escape -> back one level with focus on the trigger, Escape -> back to root (0 inert inside the menu), Escape -> menu closed, focus on hamburger, 0 `[inert]` page-wide, all `aria-expanded` false. Real mouse clicks (Playwright locator clicks) for open, submenu toggle, Back and close: same results, no focus ring after mouse. Pre-existing `inert` on the footer survived open / close; breakpoint change 390 -> 1280 with the menu open closed it with 0 inert; the search overlay was closed by opening the menu. Console: 0 errors. **Limits:** the initial focus on the hamburger was set with `element.focus()` after a real Tab press (not tabbed to); the pre-existing-inert, search-exclusivity and open / close at breakpoint checks used synthetic `.click()` / viewport emulation; no physical touch, no screen reader (VoiceOver / TalkBack), Safari / iOS not tested.

**Staging deploy:** theme `/home/dreampoint.b2b/public_html/wp-content/themes/dreampoint-b2b`; baseline hashes verified (server `_header.scss` is the CRLF copy of the HEAD content, others identical to HEAD); backup `/home/dreampoint.b2b/backups/g3g4-20261124-103729` (the four replaced files, ownership `dream9399`); installed as `dream9399:dream9399` mode 664; SHA-256 parity local = staging: `js/mobile-menu.js` `596431e3...`, `sass/components/_header.scss` `f7206833...` (CRLF, as on the server), `sass/components/_nav.scss` `7d347623...`, `style.css` `50600ca8...`. Staging Git untouched, nothing committed or pushed.

**Remaining owner manual QA:** real touch on a phone / tablet (hamburger, Search, Cart, bottom bar), Safari / iOS focus behaviour in the menu, screen-reader pass (VoiceOver / TalkBack) on the menu. Effort about 3.5 h (rounded 4 h).

Not needed: client input. No A2 implementation or transaction test has been started.
